### GATE-B-LOCAL-REGIME (2026-08-27) — THE ONLY ESCAPE LEFT BY THE GATE B THEOREM IS NOW MEASURED, ITS UPPER END IS CERTIFIED BY A THIRD BASE AT DEFECT 1336/2025, AND THE DIRECTION THAT WOULD CLOSE IT IS PROVED TO BE A FRANKL-FRONTIER PROBLEM

* **Gap chosen.** After E18 the finite input was already exact, so the highest
  remaining gap was not a certificate but the one escape the theorem leaves:
  \(c_{\rm loc}=\lim_{\varepsilon\to0^+}\sup\{-A_+/\varepsilon_\vee:
  0<\varepsilon_\vee\le\varepsilon\}\). Every negative family known before this
  round had \(\varepsilon_\vee\ge444/625\), so the regime was entirely
  unexplored.
* **Four lemmas, proved.** Failures come in pairs (\(X\cup X=X\)), so
  non-union-closed implies \(\varepsilon_\vee\ge2/m^2\) — this also sharpens a
  \(1/m^2\) remark already in `PROOF.md`. The defect multiplies through success,
  so **no Cartesian power of either base has defect below its base**: the
  mechanism proving \(c_{\rm cl}^\star=+\infty\) cannot produce a single family
  in the local regime. Hence \(-A_+/\varepsilon_\vee\le m^2\log_2m/2\) and
  defect \(\varepsilon\) forces \(m\ge\sqrt{2/\varepsilon}\). A zero-defect
  admissible family is a Frankl counterexample, and Frankl is verified for
  \(n\le12\) [REPORTED: Vučković–Živković 2017; \(m\le50\) via arXiv:1711.04276].
* **The blocker, stated exactly.** A positive universal defect floor at cap
  \(2/5\) implies every union-closed family has an element in more than \(2m/5\)
  of its sets, roughly \(0.018\) beyond the published \(\psi=0.381966\ldots\)
  frontier that this repository's Campaign I certified at \(\psi+10^{-4}\). So
  emptying the local regime by proof is a Frankl-frontier problem; this round
  measures and brackets it instead.
* **A third certified base lowers the frontier.** `verify_gate_b_rational.py`
  gained an `ExplicitBase` type (search witnesses have no weight-cell
  description, but every exact check is independent of provenance). The witness
  has 45 rows on seven coordinates, all degrees at the cap 18, incidence 126,
  exact defect \(1336/2025=0.6597\ldots\), and certified
  \(A_+\in[-0.00085518533372314171100789579,-0.00085518533372314171100789029]\),
  width \(5.5\times10^{-27}\), so \(A_+<-1/1200\). **Its automorphism group is
  trivial** — 5040 distinct enclosures, 12,716,352 Bellman states, no symmetry
  quotient possible even in principle. Verdict
  `PROVED_GATE_B_UNBOUNDED_EXACT_RATIONAL` in 159 s; a rerun resumes with
  `new_evaluations: 0`. This certifies \(e^\star\le1336/2025\), down from
  \(64/81\), and gives a third infinite construction
  (\(\varepsilon_\vee=1-(689/2025)^k\), ratio \(>k/1200\)).
* **The measurements run against a local counterexample.** Minimizing \(A_+\)
  under a hard defect cap at \(m=45\) is monotone and crosses zero between caps
  \(0.64\) and \(0.66\): \(-0.00086\) at \(0.66\), then \(+0.0117\), \(+0.0213\),
  \(+0.0440\), \(+0.0560\), \(+0.0694\), \(+0.0795\) at \(0.35\). All 38
  minimum-defect witnesses at \(n\in\{6,7\}\), \(m\ge15\) have \(A_+>0\)
  (\(+0.0588\) to \(+0.5607\)); at the two bases' own sizes the minimum-defect
  witnesses have defect \(0.2944\)/\(0.3417\) with \(A_+=+0.0588\)/\(+0.0847\)
  against the bases' \(0.7104\)/\(0.7901\) with \(-0.0137\)/\(-0.0286\). Exact
  enumeration of every admissible family at \(n\le4\) gives minimum defect
  \(2/9\); searched minima at \(n\le8\) never went below \(2/9\) although the
  pair floor \(2/m^2\) falls to \(0.0003\). So \(-A_+\) vanishes as the defect
  falls and the ratio collapses, from \(0.0363\) to \(27/21376\).
* **Two instrument failures found and fixed, both recorded.** A random-restart
  hunt over \(\lambda\in\{0.04,0.06,0.10\}\) found zero negatives in 48 restarts
  — but it never rediscovered either certified base, so it measured search
  difficulty, not the landscape (12-order screen noise against a \(0.03\)
  signal, plus infeasible starts). Replaced by a descent that starts *at* a
  certified negative with the exact all-order objective. The first sweep seeded
  every cap from the minimum-defect witness and could not climb back into the
  negativity region, reporting \(+0.037\) where a negative was feasible;
  replaced by best-known-under-cap seeding in a `sweep2:` key namespace. The
  seeder also silently dropped \(m=45\) at \(n=7\) (mean row weight \(<3\) makes
  independent column draws repeat rows), fixed by walking to admissibility from
  distinct random rows.
* **Evidence discipline.** Record schema governs *work skipping* only; a witness
  family from an older algorithm remains valid evidence once re-verified for
  admissibility and defect, with its schema of origin reported. This is a
  deliberate split (`read_checkpoint` strict, `read_witnesses` agnostic) and is
  pinned by a test.
* **Verification.** 55 tests pass in 61.8 s, including eleven new local-regime
  tests and two pinning the new certificate. One worker, `nice -n 19`, machine
  CPU below 50 % throughout; every long run resumable and append-only.
* **Open.** \(c_{\rm loc}\) is undecided. No admissible family with small defect
  and \(A_+<0\) was found; none is excluded. The certified bracket is
  \(e^\star\le1336/2025\), with nothing negative found below defect \(0.64\).


### E389-TIER-IS-FREE (2026-08-27) — THE SMALL-PRIME HALF OF ERDŐS #389 IS PROVED TO BE A DENSITY-ZERO CONSTRAINT, SO THE WHOLE PROBLEM IS NOW ONE RUN CONJECTURE, AND CONGRUENCE REPAIRS ARE PRICED OUT

* **Checkpoint reverified, and one recorded fingerprint retracted.** A fresh
  \(1\le m\le50,\ 1\le k\le50{,}000\) atlas is again semantically identical to
  the canonical artifact (6,493 near patterns, 2,249 shift patterns), the
  classification diff returns `PASS_EXACT_RECOMPUTATION` with 101,646 changed
  cases and a byte-identical ledger
  (`c646aa350229a1bdf664474c9b7091064129d2c333b0bc8fab33ed14924b4a45`), and
  `verify_known.py` re-certifies all 27 published witnesses.
  **Retraction:** the semantic SHA-256 `967bdc42…` recorded in the previous
  entry cannot be reproduced from any documented normalization — it came from an
  ad-hoc in-session computation. The fix is structural, not textual:
  `compare_atlas_classifications.semantic_sha256` now defines the normalization
  (drop exactly `generated_at_utc`, `resources`, `runtime`; canonicalize the
  rest) and every diff artifact records it. The canonical atlas digest is
  `c1d5f70d134a1544e86f000cf1c9c45df265d5778e04fef6b44cad343589505f`; the fresh
  rerun reproduces it, and two tests pin the value and prove the function
  ignores exactly the three environment blocks.
* **The small-prime tier is asymptotically free (PROVED).** Three steps, all
  elementary. (i) Digit-sum identity
  \(s_p(m,k)=(2S_p(m+k)-S_p(m+2k)-S_p(m))/(p-1)\), so the slack depends only on
  the base-\(p\) digits of \(k\). (ii) Carry bound \(s_p\ge W-D-Z\), where \(D\)
  counts \(m\)'s digits, \(Z\) is the run of digits \(p-1\) above position
  \(D\), and \(W\) counts higher digits with \(2d\ge p\); the bound is attained
  (minimum margin \(0\) over 36,400 checked triples). (iii) Counting digits
  gives a closed-form failure bound and, for \(J\ge16(D+1)\), the envelope
  \(4e^{-J/24}\) over \(k<p^J\), i.e. \(4X^{-1/(24\log p)}\).
* **Consequence: the problem is exactly one run conjecture (PROVED).** Section 9
  already split witnesses as tier \(\wedge\) run. Since tier failures have
  density zero, \(\#\{k\le X\ \text{witness}\}\ge\#(G_m\cap[1,X])-o(X)\): if the
  set of \(k\) whose whole bad window is compensation-good has positive upper
  density, that \(m\) has infinitely many witnesses. The prime-power band and
  the small-prime CRT classes are construction devices, not obstructions. With
  goodness monotone in \(m\), Conjecture R (runs of \(L\) consecutive
  \(1\)-compensation-good integers have positive upper density) implies Erdős
  #389 for every \(m\). The reduction is unconditional; only R is open.
* **Exact tier numbers, without enumeration.** A base-\(p\) digit dynamic
  program counts failures over every \(k\) in a range. For \(m=27\) the union
  over \(p\le27\) is \(0.382703\) at \(10^6\), \(0.013272\) at the published
  witness scale \(5.05\cdot10^{12}\), \(0.012370\) at \(10^{13}\); per prime it
  reaches \(3.86\cdot10^{-23}\) at \(p=2\) and \(1.20\cdot10^{-15}\) at
  \(p=23\) at ranges of 76 and 77 decimal digits, with measured decay
  exponents \(0.187\)–\(0.306\). Beyond \(X=2^{250}\), a 76-digit range, the
  exact tier density is already below the measured \((0.123664)^{14}\);
  through the provable envelope instead of the exact counts the same
  comparison needs \(2^{3343}\), a 1007-digit range. Three independent
  methods (digit program, prefix-decomposition program, direct enumeration)
  agree on 137 cells.
* **Congruence-forced mass is priced (PROVED, using Mertens).** Call
  \((M,k)\) *size-forcing* when the part of every window term pinned by
  \(k\bmod M\) already meets the powersmooth threshold of (25), so no unforced
  cofactor can be fatal. Counting forced mass against the modulus,
  \(\log M\ge\frac L4\log\frac k2-\frac L2(\log L+1)\). For \(L\ge5\) this
  forces \(M>k\) past an explicit threshold — \(k\ge70{,}247\) at \(m=27\),
  \(k\ge4{,}708{,}708\) at \(m=13\), none for \(m\le8\) — so such a class holds
  at most one integer below the target and names the witness instead of
  predicting it. At \(m=26,\ k=5{,}048{,}891{,}644{,}620\) it needs at least 30
  decimal digits against a 13-digit target. **Scope, stated explicitly:** this
  prices one pair, not a class certifying unbounded \(k\) — Section 8 already
  settles that reading by Dirichlet and empties the hypothesis, where the
  inequality would be vacuous. Over a dyadic range it has content. Small moduli
  are excluded inside a range only for \(M\le(\log N)^A\) by Siegel–Walfisz
  (or \(M\le N^{1/2-\varepsilon}\) under GRH); Linnik bounds the *least*
  prime of the cofactor progression and so places a fatal \(k\) near
  \(M^{5.2}\), before the range rather than inside it. The interval between
  those thresholds and \(N^{L/4}\) is open and recorded as such.
  **[SUPERSEDED 2026-08-27 by E389-ELEMENTARY-EVICTION: the three sentences
  above are obsolete. Section 15 evicts every class of modulus \(M\le\sqrt N/2\)
  from the block elementarily, with no prime-distribution input at all, so
  neither Siegel–Walfisz nor GRH nor Linnik is the binding tool, and the open
  interval starts at \(\sqrt N\), not at \((\log N)^A\).]**
  The
  accounting covers mirror lifts (17), the repair cone (19), and the eviction
  systems (20a)/(20b); half-window translation (23) escapes only because it
  prescribes nothing.
* **Two elementary routes to \(R(1)\) closed, with exact numbers.** Short
  cofactor failures are pairwise disjoint, so their \(\log2\) is irreducible;
  grouping level failures by usable level count and applying Mertens gives the
  closed-form limit \(\sum_{r\ge1}2^{-r}\log\frac{r+2}{r+1}=0.32252\), so the
  union total tends to \(1.01567>1\) and no union bound can prove positive
  density. It dips below one at finite scale (\(0.96981\) at \(1.2\cdot10^6\),
  \(0.98660\) at \(1.02\cdot10^7\), \(1.00659\) at \(10^{10}\)), where exact
  counting is stronger anyway. The obvious repair — keep only level failures
  outside the short-cofactor class — is **refuted**: exhaustive classification
  of every integer of a block gives densities \(0.144500\) good, \(0.629345\)
  short only, \(0.172740\) level only, \(0.053415\) both at \(10^6\), while
  independence predicts \(0.071745\) for the level-only class, a factor
  \(2.41\) low (\(2.35\) at \(10^7\)). The modes are adversely correlated: a
  prime above \(\sqrt{2w}\) leaves a cofactor below \(\sqrt{w/2}\) with few
  primes able to fail a level test. Partitioning by the largest prime factor
  removes the overlap honestly but needs equidistribution of \(p\)-smooth
  cofactors in progressions to moduli about \(\sqrt X\), which is
  Bombieri–Vinogradov-strength and not attempted.
* **Also fixed.** A mislabeled constant: the largest published witness is
  \(a(25)=5{,}048{,}891{,}644{,}621\), not \(a(26)\) (the two differ by one
  through the odd-to-even shift).
* **Verification.** 77 tests pass (22 new: 9 tier, 6 forcing, 5 decomposition,
  2 semantic digest), including DP-versus-enumeration agreement off power
  boundaries, bound and envelope dominance, an independent recomputation of the
  recorded union count at the witness scale, agreement of the compensation
  counter with the existing independent implementation, and a check that the
  greedy certifying modulus really satisfies the hypothesis of (33). New
  producers [`tier_density_dp.py`](e389/tier_density_dp.py),
  [`forcing_mass_bound.py`](e389/forcing_mass_bound.py), and
  [`good_density_decomposition.py`](e389/good_density_decomposition.py); new
  artifacts `data/tier_density_m1_30.json`, `data/forcing_mass_bound.json`,
  `data/good_density_decomposition.json`; proofs in
  [`THEOREMS.md`](e389/THEOREMS.md) Sections 13–14.
* **Costs.** Tier certificate 4.8 s, forcing certificate 2.0 s, decomposition
  certificate 1.7 s, atlas rerun and diff regeneration under 30 s combined,
  full suite 2.6 s. One process throughout, `nice` 10, machine below 25 %.

### LIU-H2-BOUNDARY-LAYER (2026-08-27) — THE ZERO-SUPPORT STRATUM OF LIU'S HYPOTHESIS 2 NOW HAS AN EXPLICIT QUANTITATIVE ESTIMATE, AND IT IS NOT THE BINDING ONE

* **Named obligation.** `uc/liu9_tube.py` refuses to certify any tube radius
  and names three missing ingredients, the second being "an explicit
  \(y\log(1/y)\) boundary-layer estimate covering the zero-mass strata rather
  than Taylor-expanding through them".  `uc/liu9_binding.py` already certified
  the *leading coefficient* \(B=2px[1+\beta(1-x)]-1>0\) at the binding
  distribution and \(V(y)\ge0\) on \([0,1]\), but both are statements at one
  point: no threshold, no uniform constant, no inequality at a general
  nine-parameter point.  The 2026-08-27 residual localization made this the
  highest-value target — 70 of 79 surviving complement boxes touch \(b_j=0\).
* **Exact derivative identity (PROVED, cross-checked two ways).**  Writing the
  denominator-cleared gap as
  \(\mathrm{gap}=(1-\beta)\mathrm{EHXY}+\beta\,\mathrm{EHPI}-\mathrm{EHX}\),
  differentiation of the three sums gives
  \(\partial\,\mathrm{gap}/\partial b_j=w_jG_j(V)\) with
  $$G_j=2(1-\beta)\sum_k w_kb_kh'(b_jb_k)+2\beta\!\!\sum_{k\in\mathrm{comp}(j)}\!\!a_k\pi_1(b_j,b_k)h'(\pi(b_j,b_k))-h'(b_j).$$
  Two independent controls: forward-mode differentiation *through Liu's own
  transcribed `_formula`* agrees with the closed form to \(1.2\times10^{-60}\)
  over 3,240 stratum points; and the finite difference
  \(\Delta G/\Delta\log(1/y)\) between \(y=10^{-40}\) and \(10^{-80}\)
  reproduces the independently Arb-certified \(B\) to 41 digits
  (\(-3.93\times10^{-41}\)).
* **The estimate (PROVED, Arb constants).**  For any point of the raw box with
  \(0<b_j\le y_0\le1/4\),
  \(G_j\ge[2(1-\beta)\mu-1]\log(1/b_j)-2(1-\beta)\log\frac1{1-y_0}\), using
  only \(b_k\le1\), \(y b_k\le y_0\) and \(\pi\le2y_0\le1/2\).  Integrating
  along \(b_j\downarrow0\) — the total mean drop over *all* layer coordinates
  is at most \(y_0\sum_jw_j\le y_0\) — gives, at \(y_0=1/32\),
  $$\mathrm{gap}(V)\ \ge\ \mathrm{gap}(V|_{\text{layer}:=0})+m_0\sum_{j\in S}w_jb_j,\qquad m_0\in[0.1876317632\ldots],$$
  with the sharper single-coordinate constant
  \(m_0^{\rm s}\in[0.2991615718\ldots]\).  So the layer is *reduced to the
  face*, where \(|h'''|\le1022.93\ldots\) is finite for supports in
  \([y_0,1-y_0]\): the impossible cubic remainder is never needed on this
  stratum.
* **It is not the binding stratum.**  Zeroing the layer moves the tube distance
  by at most \((2\delta+y_0x^2)\sum_jw_jb_j\), so the layer is
  \(\kappa\)-monotone for \(\kappa\le m_0/(2\delta+y_0x^2)\): \(0.8730627\)
  at \(\delta\le1/10\) and \(5.374\) at \(\delta\le1/100\), against the smooth
  \(A/C\) ceiling \(0.7004675\) recomputed here from the certified \(A\) and
  \(C\).  Since \(\delta_c\le\rho/\sqrt{\min(q,1-q)}\) in a radius-\(\rho\)
  tube, the layer constant decays exactly as \(q\to\{0,1\}\) — which explains
  the measured 57-of-70 overlap between the zero-support and \(q\)-degenerate
  survivors.  Ingredients (i) and (iii) are untouched and (ii)+(iii) are
  coupled.
* **A mirror stratum nobody had named (PROVED, measured).**  The face is only
  \(C^3\) for supports in \([y_0,1-y_0]\): \(h'''\) blows up at \(b_j\to1\)
  too, and a small-mass atom near 1 is inside the tube.  Redoing the singular
  bookkeeping there, only partners with \(b_k=1\) contribute, through
  \(h'(b_j\cdot1)\) and \(\pi(b_j,1)=b_j\), giving the coefficient
  \(1-2(1-\beta)W_1-2\beta A_1\) with \(W_1\) the weight already at support
  1.  The slope in \(\log(1/(1-b_j))\) between \(1-10^{-40}\) and
  \(1-10^{-80}\) matches that prediction to \(10^{-40}\) at six
  configurations, including the discriminating \(W_1=\tfrac12,A_1=1\) case
  where it equals \(-\beta\) to twenty digits.  Unlike the lower layer the
  mean constraint does not sign it: the gap increases toward 1 for
  \(W_1<\tfrac12\) and decreases for \(W_1>\tfrac12\).  This stratum is
  **OPEN** and is not claimed here.
* **Threshold trade-off (PROVED, Arb).**  \(y_0=1/8\) and \(1/16\) are refuted
  (\(\Lambda<0\)); \(1/24\) through \(1/1024\) are certified, with \(m_0\)
  rising \(0.074\to0.865\) while the face's \(|h'''|\) ceiling rises
  \(575\to1.05\times10^6\).  That is the quantitative trade-off a piecewise
  lemma has to price.
* **Verified.** `uc/liu9_boundary_layer.py` PASS in 51.2 s single core:
  57,600 dyadic cells for the cross link (exactly 1 tight at the corner
  \((y_0,1)\) where the bound is an equality), 160,000 cells for the paired
  link, 3,240 stratum points (240 structured corners at \(q\in\{0,10^{-9},
  \tfrac12,1-10^{-9},1\}\) and \(b_j\) down to \(10^{-30}\), plus 3,000 random
  draws), zero failures, worst single-coordinate actual/predicted ratio 2.61.
  `uc/verification/test_liu9_boundary_layer.py` 9/9: it rejects \(y_0=1/8\)
  and \(1/16\), a dropped \(-h'(y)\), a halved cross term, a \(y_0>1/4\)
  paired discard, and a tenfold inflated \(m_0\).  Report
  [`liu9-boundary-layer.json`](uc/verification/results/liu9-boundary-layer.json).
* **Single source of truth.** `uc/liu9_tube.py` no longer advertises (ii) as
  missing: its local-analysis section, its `LocalAttempt.blocker`, and its
  verdict now record (ii) as supplied and name (i) and (iii) as what remains.

### GATE-B-EXACT-RATIONAL-CERTIFICATE (2026-08-27) — THE FINITE INPUT TO GATE B IS NOW AN EXACT RATIONAL FACT ABOUT THE TRUE OBJECTIVE, AND THE DIVERGENCE IS PINNED TO ORDER $n$

* **Gap that mattered.** Gate B's conclusion \(c_{\rm cl}^\star=+\infty\) was
  already proved, but its only numerical input — the sign of \(A_+\) on the two
  bases — rested on two *numerical* stacks. The Arb route needs `python-flint`
  plus a three-way clamp classification; the dyadic route needs a 160-bit
  interval class, hand-derived atanh/exp tail bounds, and it *relaxes every
  feasible action interval to \([0,1]\)*, so it certifies a relaxation of
  \(C_+\) rather than \(C_+\). Neither could separate "the true optimum is
  negative" from "some upper bound of it is negative".
* **Exact rational certificate (CERTIFIED EXACTLY).**
  `uc/gate_b/verify_gate_b_rational.py` evaluates the one-sided causal
  recurrence over the **exact** feasible interval \([s^*(p,r),U(p,r)]\) in
  ordinary Python fractions and returns a two-sided enclosure. Three certified
  primitives suffice, all integer arithmetic: bit-by-bit \(\log_2\) from
  \(\log_2y=(b+\log_2(y^2/2^b))/2\) with monotone state rounding and tail in
  \([0,2^{-N}]\); ceiling square-root towers \(r_j\ge2^{2^{-j}}\) for \(2^x\);
  and a concavity case split for \(\max_{[\ell,u]}[h(s)+cs]\), exact at an
  endpoint and otherwise the Fenchel value \(\log_2(1+2^c)\). Over **all** 720
  \(n=6\) and **all** 5040 \(n=7\) orders (584,784 and 13,381,680 Bellman
  states, no automorphism quotient):
  $$
  A_+(\mathcal D)\in[-0.013672107732177773561859915661,
  -0.013672107732177773561859912998],
  $$
  $$
  A_+(\mathcal B)\in[-0.028649186794468317816026981933,
  -0.028649186794468317816026976455],
  $$
  widths \(2.7\times10^{-27}\) and \(5.5\times10^{-27}\). The upper endpoints
  re-derive the sharp \(-17/1250\) and \(-7/250\) bounds from one
  standard-library artifact, and both Arb values from E2/E17 lie strictly
  inside, so retaining unconstrained maxima at the 982 and 305 ambiguous clamp
  states cost under \(10^{-26}\). Runtime 250.35 s in one `nice -n 10` process
  at 2.4 % of machine CPU; 5,760 resumable schema-checked records.
* **Symmetry recovered, not assumed.** The all-order value lists partition into
  10 classes of size 72 and 21 classes of size 240, independently reproducing
  the 72- and 240-element automorphism groups.
* **Product lemmas re-attacked exactly.** The old 292-case tensorization search
  was float64 (worst gap \(1.8\times10^{-15}\)), which cannot distinguish exact
  additivity from additivity up to machine epsilon.
  `uc/gate_b/audit_tensorization_exact.py` repeats it with certified rational
  enclosures on 35 ordered products — 20 with unequal block dimensions,
  including singletons, full power sets, dead and duplicate columns,
  deterministic coordinates, union-closed against non-union-closed factors, and
  seeded random pairs — over **every** global order, 15,010 in total, demanding
  that the two enclosures intersect. Result: zero intersection failures, zero
  exact defect-identity failures, worst \(Q\) discrepancy \(1.4\times10^{-27}\),
  and worst fixed-order Bellman discrepancy **exactly zero**. A complete resume
  reports `new_evaluations: 0`.
* **Growth rate pinned (PROVED).** With \(c_{\rm cl}^\star(n)\) the supremum
  over admissible families on at most \(n\) coordinates, powers of the bases
  give \(c_{\rm cl}^\star(n)>n/250-7/250\) and \(>17n/7500-17/1250\). In the
  other direction every local \(Q\) term is a binary entropy and \(C_+\) is a
  maximum of sums of binary entropies, so \(Q\ge0\), \(C_+\ge0\), hence
  \(A_+\ge-\log_2m\ge-n\) and \(-A_+/\varepsilon_\vee\le n/\varepsilon_0\) once
  \(\varepsilon_\vee\ge\varepsilon_0\). The Gate B ratio is therefore
  \(\Theta(n)\) under a positive defect floor: the divergence is the true
  growth rate up to a constant, and the only escape is
  \(\varepsilon_\vee\to0\) — precisely the separate local-stability question.
* **Suite and paper.** 42 tests pass in 60.2 s, including Arb-versus-rational
  bracketing of every primitive, 65-point sampled domination of the constrained
  maximum, per-orbit enclosure agreement on both bases, sharpness against the
  dyadic relaxation, state-canonicalization invariance, orbit-versus-all-order
  certificate equality, foreign-schema and truncated-checkpoint rejection, an
  exact one-case audit replay, and the exact-integer growth corollary. The
  paper draft now builds to eight pages with the new proposition, the growth
  section, and the exact control.
* **Costs.** Canonical rational run 250.35 s; exact tensorization audit 62.2 s
  then 0.09 s on a complete resume; suite 60.2 s. One worker throughout, `nice`
  10 or 19, never above 2.4 % of total machine CPU.

### E389-HALF-WINDOW-EVICTION-DENSITY (2026-08-27) — EVICTION TURNS OUT TO BE FREE, THE WITNESS DENSITY GETS AN UNCONDITIONAL CEILING, AND THE \(m=27\) SHIFT NEIGHBOURHOOD REACHES \(10^9\)

* **Checkpoint reverified before extension.** A fresh
  \(1\le m\le50,\ 1\le k\le50{,}000\) atlas is semantically identical to the
  canonical schema-2 artifact after stripping time/resource metadata (6,493
  near patterns, 2,249 shift patterns), the
  independent classification diff again returns `PASS_EXACT_RECOMPUTATION` with
  101,646 changed cases and a **byte-identical** 21.9 MB ledger
  (`c646aa350229a1bdf664474c9b7091064129d2c333b0bc8fab33ed14924b4a45`), and the
  targeted collision regression passes.
* **Half-window translation evicts every large blocker (PROVED, SHARP).** For
  \(L=\lceil m/2\rceil\), if \(p>m\) divides a term of \(\mathcal B(m,k)\) then
  \(p\) divides no term of \(\mathcal B(m,k+L)\): the two windows are adjacent
  blocks spanning at most \(m\) consecutive integers, shorter than the gap
  between multiples of any \(p>m\). Every smaller offset \(1\le\delta\le L-1\)
  has an explicit surviving blocker (\(i_p=\lfloor m/2\rfloor+1+\delta\),
  \(k=p-i_p\)), so \(L\) is the least uniform offset. No modulus, no product, no
  CRT.
* **The digit explosion was an artifact, and 12 open cases closed.** The
  49--98-digit zero-carry and 32--81-digit adaptive candidates came from
  demanding the *sufficient* uniform classes (15) instead of the exact
  small-prime tier. All 12 recorded \(m=27\) survivor systems satisfy the exact
  tier at \(k+14\) with **no search**: 13-digit candidates, all 140 old
  blockers evicted over 1,960 checks, inside the factorization bound, windows
  factored completely (369 large-prime factors) and every one classified
  `new_large_prime_only` with 9--15 exact obstructions. The 11 previously
  unclassified cases are resolved. Certificate
  `data/exact_eviction_m1_20_k5000_m27.json`
  (`8eaad3f1e3521f238dcd9c8451ab66744a7c23010d9b4a0e6ebfa1972db71130`) also
  verifies the lemma at 587,169 attached primes / 3,871,667 divisibility checks,
  sharpness for every \(m\le200\) (9,900 instances), and classifies all 98,706
  large-prime-obstructed sources of \(m\le20,\ k\le5{,}000\): 1,033 witnesses,
  59,641 new-prime-only, 162 small-prime-only, 37,870 both, with 9,832 full
  Legendre/Kummer cross-checks.
* **Unconditional density ceiling (PROVED, via PNT).** Compensation of
  \(p^e\Vert w\) forces \(p^{e}<\sqrt{2w}\), so a prime factor \(p>m\) with
  \(p^2>2w\) is always fatal. Counting short-cofactor integers by cofactor gives
  \(S(X)\sim X\log 2\), hence
  \(\#\{k\le X:(m,k)\text{ witness}\}\le(1-\log2+o(1))X\) for every fixed \(m\)
  — the first density statement in the file. Moreover the repair window for a
  dominant prime is only \(p\in(\sqrt w,\sqrt{2w})\), of density
  \(O(1/\log X)\): the \(\log 2\) proportion is lost, not repaired. Certificate
  `data/smooth_density_m1_20_k3000.json`
  (`624432944d2146be92b2ca3a94813a6e5f4f76e5b827c4b119a0b66f4fb17931`) checks
  331,361 prime powers, two independent counts of \(S(X)\) at
  \(X=10^5,10^6,2\cdot10^6\) (densities 0.67277, 0.679867, 0.68121), and the
  narrow window (0.06044, 0.051792, 0.0493005).
* **One integer decides every shift (PROVED).** Goodness is monotone in \(m\),
  so with the window identity a witness source shifts as follows: odd \(m\)
  keeps its window and can fail only at \(p\le m+1\); even \(m\) adjoins exactly
  \(x/2\) and fails at a large prime iff that one integer is not
  \((m+1)\)-compensation-good. `data/parity_shift_m1_20_k4000_published.json`
  (`cffbaafa9b83f2c1328e4a81389b0d5bd4bd20659f03dab028387e0ec6a036d0`) confirms
  this at 905 rectangle sources and all 26 published witnesses: 433/435
  odd-source shifts are witnesses (2 small-prime failures), 378 of 470
  even-source shifts fail at the adjoined term alone, all 13 published
  odd-source shifts succeed, and all 13 even-source shifts fail. At \(m=26\) the
  adjoined term is the prime \(5{,}048{,}891{,}644{,}633\), which alone accounts
  for the failure at the first unlisted index.
* **A measurement bias caught and turned into a control.** A block starting at
  \(5{,}048{,}891{,}644{,}635\) showed a run of 12 where the independence model
  expects \(2\cdot10^{-6}\). Cause: that block starts inside the published
  \(m=25\) witness window, whose 13 terms are 25-good hence 27-good. The
  certificate now asserts the full run of 13 as a labelled control beside an
  unbiased block whose longest run is 5. The published data already supplies 13
  of the 14 integers \(m=27\) needs.
* **Shift-target neighbourhood extended fivefold.** Four contiguous exact
  compensation-run shards from \(k=5{,}049{,}091{,}644{,}619\) add
  \(8\cdot10^8\) candidates, each rejecting all \(2\cdot10^8\) offsets by an
  exact negative local slack at some \(p>27\) with no offset reaching the
  small-prime tier; longest runs 8, 9, 9, 8. With the two moving artifacts the
  union is contiguous: **no \(m=27\) witness for
  \(5{,}048{,}891{,}644{,}619\le k<5{,}049{,}891{,}644{,}619\)**, exactly
  \(10^9\) candidates. Shard SHA-256:
  `6a43bb2ab3602d971b9390422e69098e61558ba2617c4bad9461b3f2879f1301`,
  `1347f8c7d15752d05d3fb6fc55fc01805978449b405c58e1a52ed6bdbd82b3fd`,
  `f88709c4877ad631f0a1f7e43510d831771856b8c357d0086837e04933f914e6`,
  `88e59dc5145a92ae68584a03f56e453cca86a89eccf6664a312bf08a28bf898b`.
* **Quantitative barrier and cost, NOT proved.** Measured compensation-good
  density is 0.142756 near \(10^6\), 0.124948 near \(10^9\), 0.123664 near
  \(5\cdot10^{12}\), and 0.123936 across the new shards; the exact small-prime
  tier passes for 19,920 of 20,000 consecutive \(k\). Run spectra track the
  independence model within one unit of the longest run. Regressing
  \(\log k_m\) on \(\lceil m/2\rceil\) over the 26 published witnesses gives
  \(R^2=0.989\), implied per-term density 0.1119, and predicted least \(m=27\)
  witness \(2.8\cdot10^{13}\); the independence model predicts
  \(5.1\cdot10^{12}\). At the measured \(2.8\cdot10^5\) candidates/s this is
  \(1.8\cdot10^7\) single-core seconds — expensive but not out of reach.
* **Verification and cost.** 55 tests pass in 1.75 s, including 12 new
  regressions for the eviction lemma and its sharpness, the powersmooth bound
  and its attained minimum, the narrow repair window, three independent
  short-cofactor counts, the published-window run of 13, the shift reduction,
  and shard contiguity. Every producer ran as one reduced-priority process:
  5.48 s, 3.29 s, and 0.71 s for the three new certificates, plus
  717.99--725.21 s for each shard. Machine CPU sampled 44--48% including other
  campaigns, under the 50% ceiling.
* **Status and pivot.** Erdős #389 remains open; no novelty claim is made. Old
  blockers are no longer part of the difficulty and the small-prime tier is
  almost never binding, so the entire remaining problem is the existence of
  \(\lceil m/2\rceil\) consecutive \(m\)-compensation-good integers — by (25) a
  correlation problem for large prime factors of consecutive integers, for which
  the elementary bounds used here degrade past two terms.

### UC-BRIDGE-EXHAUSTION-CAMPAIGN-K-RESUME (2026-08-27) — THE ENTROPY BRIDGE IS NOW EXHAUSTIVELY MACHINE-CHECKED, A SECOND REFEREE FINDS NO PROOF DEFECT, AND CAMPAIGN K'S THREE LOST SLICES ARE RUNNING AGAIN

* **The entropy-to-union-closed bridge is exhaustively machine-checked for
  \(n\le4\).** `uc/verification/entropy_bridge_exhaustive.py` rebuilds the
  sequential coupling from the statement of Corollary `cor:uc` alone,
  importing no campaign snapshot, no certificate module, and not
  `uc/bridge_uc.py`.  It runs on **every** nonempty family of subsets of
  \([n]\), \(n\le4\): 65,808 families and 1,631,880 coupled prefix states, 48.2 s
  on one core.  Exact rational arithmetic checks the four coupling masses,
  both Bernoulli marginals, \(\Pr(A_i\vee C_i\mid\text{prefixes})=s^\star\),
  prefix-by-prefix uniformity of \(A\) and \(C\),
  \(\mathcal L(P_i)=\mathcal L(R_i)\) with mean the frequency of element \(i\),
  and the chain rule \(\mathsf H(A)=\sum_i\mathbb E\,h(P_i)\).  256-bit Arb
  certifies both data-processing inequalities: every enclosure is provably
  nonnegative or a structural tie inside \(\pm2^{-200}\).  All 5,096 enumerated
  union-closed families satisfy the \(t_{\rm cert}\) conclusion, the extreme
  case being exactly \(1/2\).  Report
  `uc/verification/results/entropy-bridge-exhaustive.json`, canonical SHA-256
  `6681f8faf13d9344f8e7bf3a5d7785ed289f1e82c5e9badd1e7967c03e3ebfef`.
* **The control is not vacuous.**
  `uc/verification/test_entropy_bridge_exhaustive.py` passes 4/4: dropping the
  \(\max\) branch of \(s^\star\), forcing a constant prefix probability, or
  inflating the union bit each makes the exhaustion reject, on the second
  family in the first two cases.  Replacing the \(1/2\) clip by another
  admissible cap does **not** make it reject — correctly, since that coupling
  is still valid; the identification with Cambie's \(s^\star\) is the separate
  three-case proof in `bridge_uc.py`, whose own exhaustive control was extended
  to build the sequential coupling on all 65,808 families as well.
* **Second independent referee: no proof defect, no blocker.** A read-only
  audit re-derived the two links that were still human-only — the sequential
  entropy bridge and the fold/unfold plus Theorem B\('''\) support reduction —
  and confirmed the \(s^\star\) bounds, marginal preservation, the direction of
  both conditioning inequalities, coordinatewise applicability, first-coordinate
  strictness, both directions of the \(\inf F=\inf\Phi\) collapse, the
  concavity sign, the Bauer direction, and the one-moment support bound.  Eight
  minor exposition defects were found and all eight repaired in
  `uc/paper/main.tex`: empty prefix at coordinate 1; the enabling inequality
  \((1-\psi)^2=\psi\Rightarrow2u-u^2\ge1-\psi>\tfrac12\) behind \(g'<0\); the
  previously unstated three-case \(s^\star\) split; span-versus-pure-tensors in
  Stone--Weierstrass; a direct continuity route for \(\Phi_\alpha\); the
  explicit PSD cross-term derivation of convexity of \(\Ee\); the
  \(\Phi_\alpha\)-versus-\(F\) sink-law distinction; and exclusion of the empty
  family.  The manuscript rebuilds at 36 pages with no undefined reference or
  citation.  Report
  `uc/verification/results/uc-bridge-referee-2026-08-27.md`.
* **Campaign K resumed rather than restarted.** Its frozen collector rejected
  with exactly four problems, all of them the missing slice-10/12/13 result and
  trace pairs; 13 slices are committed COMPLETE with 426,828,869 boxes and
  residual = stack = `budget_time` = 0.  Because no committed artifact exists
  for those three runs and both `install_no_replace` and `write_new_bytes`
  refuse to replace one, re-running them under the same launch manifest is
  protocol-sound and preserves the 427 M boxes already paid for; creating a new
  campaign would discard all of it.  All three supervisors are live with the
  72 h per-slice budget.  Shallow-phase throughput with three resident workers
  is at least \(3.3\times10^3\) boxes/s each (on-disk trace growth over 39 min;
  buffered, so a lower bound), against a historical quiet-machine shallow rate
  near \(1.1\times10^3\) and a degraded \(4.4\times10^2\) under the original
  16-worker load.  That is explicitly *not* a completion estimate: per-box cost
  collapses with depth (about \(7\times10^1\) boxes/s in a measured deep tail),
  and the interrupted runs had each reached roughly \(10^8\) boxes without
  terminating.  The 72 h wall remains the operative bound.
* **Liu H2: the tube complement does not close, and its obstruction is now
  named box by box.** Three `uc/liu9_tube.py`/`uc/liu9_residual.py` runs at
  \(\rho=0.1\), single core, deterministic box-budget stops:
  26,247 processed leaves 71 residual; 866,551 leaves **76**; 3,000,001
  (1,500,000 splits, depth 89, 2,014 s) leaves **79**.  A 114-fold increase in
  processed boxes moves the frontier by eight boxes, so the earlier
  "optimistic total \(2.7\times10^4\) boxes" extrapolation is **refuted** as a
  completion estimate: subdivision does not clear this set.  The 866,551-box
  rerun reproduced the timed run exactly (processed, splits, mean-infeasible
  207,132, tube-out 161,583, obj-clear 64,485, residual 76, depth 81), which
  fixes reproducibility of the frontier.
* **Where the frontier lives.** Of the 79 residual boxes at 3 M,
  **70 touch the entropy-singular support corner** \(b_j=0\) (per coordinate:
  b0 23, b2 28, b4 43, b1 24, b3 29, b5 31), and **57 of those also pin
  \(q\) at a degenerate endpoint** \(\{0,1\}\), where the transverse curvature
  \(Cq(1-q)\) vanishes; no box is \(q\)-degenerate without also touching a zero
  support.  The remaining 9 are, coordinate for coordinate, the *same* nine wide boxes in both localized runs
  (width \(1/4\), depth 24, \(q\in[1/4,1/2]\), wholly outside the tube, gap
  \(\approx-1.1\)): the priority order never reaches them, so they are backlog,
  not a barrier.  The surviving obstruction is therefore exactly the two strata
  the conjectured piecewise local lemma was written for — the \(y\log(1/y)\)
  boundary layer and the \(q\in\{0,1\}\) endpoints covered by the proved
  \(D(Q)\ge0\) — rather than a diffuse nine-dimensional volume.  The local side
  is unchanged and still blocking: \(h'''([0,\rho])\) is non-finite at every
  tested radius, so no one-piece cubic tube bound exists.  Reports:
  `uc/verification/results/liu9-complement-residual-rho0.1.json` and
  `...-3M.json`.
* **External delegation blocked.** The Claude `fable` model returned
  `You've reached your Fable 5 limit` on both an isolated and a workspace
  invocation, so this session's independent referee ran as a local read-only
  subagent instead.  Model review of either kind is still not peer review.

### E389-COMPENSATION-RUN-BOUND (2026-08-26) — EXACT LOCAL DECOMPOSITION LIFTS THE \(m=27\) LOWER BOUND ABOVE \(10^9\)

* **Checkpoint reverified before extension.** A fresh
  \(1\le m\le50,\ 1\le k\le50{,}000\) atlas is semantically identical to the
  repaired schema-2 artifact after removing time/resource metadata: 6,493
  near patterns and 2,249 shift patterns. The independent classification
  comparison again passes with 101,646 changed cases, and its 21.9 MB JSONL
  ledger is byte-identical. The then-current 27-test suite passed.
* **Large primes now separate exactly by bad-window integer (PROVED).** For
  \(p^e\Vert w\) with \(p>m\), define
  \(C_p(w)=\#\{r\ge1:2((w/p^e)\bmod p^r)>p^r\}\). A pair is a witness exactly
  when every \(p\le m\) slack is nonnegative and every bad-window integer
  satisfies \(C_p(w)\ge e\) for each of its prime divisors \(p>m\). Thus the
  construction target is an exact small-prime band class intersecting a run
  of \(\lceil m/2\rceil\) compensation-good integers.
* **CRT repair family enlarged, then sharply demoted (PROVED).** For odd
  \(p\), the cofactor residues whose first \(e\) prefixes guarantee repair
  form a digit box of exact size
  \(\frac{p-1}{2}(\frac{p+1}{2})^{e-1}\), strictly enlarging the single mirror
  lift. But if a repair retains attached primes \(S\), its displacement is
  divisible by \(\prod_{p\in S}p\). At the first three \(m=27\) survivors
  those products have 69, 30, and 35 digits; at the next nine they have
  36--79 digits. A near repair must drop old blockers and face a new window.
* **Prime-power spikes unified with the local criterion (PROVED).** For an
  even-source natural shift, the target bad window is the old window with
  \(x/2\) adjoined as its first term. The exact odd-prime spike digit sum is
  precisely formula \(C_p(x/2)-v_p(x)\). The certificate checks this identity
  at 19,654 primes, the prefix cone at 19,804 residues, and the complete
  small/large separation at 100,000 pairs and 587,169 large-prime factors.
* **Natural-shift neighborhood doubled.** The next 100,000,000 offsets have
  99,999,991 single-level rejections and nine full-check survivors; seven
  fail only at large primes and two also at \(p=7\). Combined with the earlier
  shard, the first 200,000,000 forward offsets contain no witness. Moving
  artifact SHA-256:
  `ac9b02929f6fb4305ad54faef7c893f9c259d1e371d57753a74392f2641720c2`;
  combined structure SHA-256:
  `fed9bc832f21446bd11c67089acf950e37b5c004a8ef31067149583492e65daa`.
* **Global \(m=27\) lower bound advanced exactly.** The new segmented
  compensation-run sieve exhausts the contiguous interval
  \(50{,}001\le k\le1{,}000{,}050{,}000\). Every one of the \(10^9\)
  candidates has negative local slack at a prime \(p>27\); the large-prime
  condition rejects all of them before a small-prime filter is needed. The
  longest compensation-good integer run is
  9, versus the required 14. With the atlas,
  \(k_{\min}(27)>1{,}000{,}050{,}000\). Shard SHA-256 values:
  `edcc1956d8d94a50ae9d7d02ed5867cdb3d880ab9e2fc697350bb6f126b3be15`
  and `54ccb015217085e362ea4faafd4c7e98890555916028df03ce1d5c057db67666`.
* **Verification and cost.** Thirty-five tests pass in 1.216 s; targeted
  theorem, artifact, Legendre/Kummer, and published-minimum checks all pass.
  An independent code-review agent returned `PASS`. Every producer used one
  reduced-priority process with numerical thread pools pinned to one. Wall
  times were 526.59 s (second moving 100M), 6.14 s (combined structure),
  195.09 s (first global 100M), and 1,930.58 s (next global 900M).
* **Status and pivot.** Erdős #389 remains open; no novelty claim is made.
  Fixed CRT classes, same-blocker CRT repair, and finite sieving do not provide
  existence. The next proof target is an adaptive theorem producing a
  length-14 compensation-good run that also lands in one exact small-prime
  band class.

### UC-GATE-B-SECOND-BASE (2026-08-26) — AN INDEPENDENT \(n=6\) POWER CONSTRUCTION REMOVES CANDIDATE DEPENDENCE

* **Single remaining robustness gap.** The Arb and dyadic certificates were
  arithmetically disjoint but both reconstructed the same 45-row \(n=7\)
  family and used its 21-order quotient. A candidate-specific reconstruction or
  orbit error could therefore invalidate both finite inputs at once.
* **Second exact base.** On a \(3+3\) coordinate partition, let
  \(\mathcal D\) be the union of cells
  \((0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,2),(2,1)\). It has 25 distinct rows, every coordinate
  count 10, incidence 60 against exact Reimer threshold 59, active/distinct
  columns, and 444 missing ordered joins out of 625. An independent cell-weight
  multinomial calculation gives the same 181 successful joins, so
  \(1-\varepsilon_\vee(\mathcal D)=181/625\).
* **All-order dependency-free certificate.**
  `uc/gate_b/verify_gate_b_n6_dyadic.py` evaluates all \(6!=720\) orders without
  a symmetry quotient using the clamp-free 160-bit dyadic relaxation. It
  certifies
  $$
  Q(\mathcal D)<4.616383859857110260038923460896,\quad
  \mathbb E_\pi W_\pi<5.034353951629763221689815640761,
  $$
  $$
  \boxed{A_+(\mathcal D)<-0.0125897106568747589615082453240<-\tfrac1{80}}.
  $$
  The 720 roots form 10 exact interval classes of multiplicity 72, but that
  equality is checked only after every order is evaluated.
* **Arithmetically disjoint certificate.**
  `uc/gate_b/verify_gate_b_n6_arb.py` reconstructs the same cells separately,
  verifies the exact 72-element automorphism group, and passes the 10 order
  representatives through the generic 256-bit Arb recurrence. It proves
  \(A_+(\mathcal D)<-17/1250\) and writes a deterministic certificate in
  0.21 s. The older independently implemented \(n=6\) falsifier also replayed
  all 720 float policies and gave the consistent Arb enclosure
  \(-0.0136721077321777735\ldots\).
* **Second infinite construction (PROVED).** For
  \(\mathcal G_k=\mathcal D^{\boxtimes k}\),
  $$
  A_+(\mathcal G_k)=kA_+(\mathcal D),\qquad
  \varepsilon_\vee(\mathcal G_k)=1-(181/625)^k.
  $$
  Every coordinate count is \(10\cdot25^{k-1}=(2/5)25^k\); average row size is
  \(12k/5\), and \(25^5<2^{24}\) proves strict Reimer for every \(k\). Thus
  \[
  -A_+(\mathcal G_k)/\varepsilon_\vee(\mathcal G_k)>k/80\to\infty.
  \]
  This route alone proves \(c_{\rm cl}^\star=\infty\).
* **Specific counterexample attack.** A resumable direct audit materialized the
  625-row square as plain 12-bit masks, directly counted
  \(357864/390625=1-(181/625)^2\), and ran generic evaluators on five hostile
  global orders. Bellman product gap was zero throughout; worst iid gap was
  \(1.78\times10^{-15}\). The 13.20 s search resumed with zero new evaluations
  and byte-identical artifacts. The 292-case product search also resumed with
  zero counterexamples and zero exact-defect failures.
* **Adversarial controls.** Complete \(n=4\) replay retained 358 admissible
  families with zero negative values (24.11 s); complete \(n=5\) resumed all
  5,172 orbits representing 463,343 labeled families with zero new evaluations
  and minimum \(A_+=0.006474313148642441\ldots\). Exact integer tests reduce the
  second all-\(k\) cap/Reimer statement to \(25^5<2^{24}\) and verify positive
  defect and divergent ratio without finite-\(n\) extrapolation.
* **Defects found and fixed.** Isolated Python `-I` initially could not locate
  the sibling dyadic arithmetic module; the checker now inserts its resolved
  directory and both clean certificates reproduce byte-identically. The shared
  dyadic evaluator now rejects malformed orders and out-of-dimension rows.
  The new append-only square audit ignores a truncated final checkpoint line
  while still rejecting earlier corruption.
* **Verification and scope.** The expanded regression suite passes 29 tests in
  54.930 s test time / 55.84 s wall. The paper now contains
  both constructions, four certificate boundaries, both non-factored square
  controls, and the explicit limitation that both defects tend to one; it
  rebuilds to seven pages. Neither base is union-closed, and the separate
  \(\varepsilon_\vee\to0\) stability question remains open.

### UC-GATE-B-INDEPENDENCE (2026-08-26) — THE OBSTRUCTION SURVIVES REMOVING ARB, THE CLAMPS, AND THE FACTORED SQUARE

* **Gap that mattered.** Gate B's conclusion rested numerically on one
  arithmetic stack (`python-flint` Arb) and one Bellman implementation whose
  clamp classification chooses among three formulas per state. Nothing in the
  repository would have caught an inverted clamp comparison or a library bug.
  The theorem needs only \(A_+(\mathcal B)<0\), so that single point of failure
  was the highest-value target.
* **Second, dependency-free certificate (INDEPENDENTLY CERTIFIED).**
  `uc/gate_b/verify_gate_b_dyadic.py` imports nothing outside the standard
  library. It uses \(2^{-160}\) fixed-point dyadic intervals with explicit
  outward rounding, a 64-term rational atanh series with exact tail bound for
  \(\ln\), and range reduction plus a 32-term Taylor tail bound and repeated
  squaring for \(\exp\). It also discards every clamp decision by relaxing each
  nondegenerate action interval to \([0,1]\), a superset of the feasible
  one-sided interval, so its Bellman value is an upper bound by pure set
  inclusion. It proves
  $$
  Q(\mathcal B)<5.4473549773803219881606998844190,\quad
  \mathbb E_\pi W_\pi<5.9338850557040829988319350235433,
  $$
  $$
  \boxed{A_+(\mathcal B)<-0.0271742911034863966862278304907<-\tfrac1{40}}
  $$
  hence ratio \(>k/40\to\infty\) with no Arb input. The pristine system
  interpreter, no venv and no packages, reproduced
  `certificates/gate_b_unbounded_dyadic_v1.json` byte-identically in 4.02 s.
  A fresh `python-flint==0.6.0` Python 3.9 environment also reproduced
  `gate_b_unbounded_arb_v4.json` byte-identically.
* **Cross-implementation agreement.** The dyadic \(Q\) interval strictly
  contains the Arb \(Q\) ball; dyadic \(\ln\)/\(\exp\) enclosures strictly
  contain the Arb ones on sampled points; and the relaxed per-order value
  strictly exceeds the Arb one-sided certificate for all 21 order orbits. The
  240-element automorphism group, its 21 equal orbits covering all 5,040
  orders, and value invariance along orbits are now checked rather than assumed.
* **Falsification searches found nothing (NEW ADVERSARIAL EVIDENCE).**
  `audit_tensorization.py` evaluated 292 ordered products in 66.41 s, 205 with
  unequal block dimensions, including dead columns, duplicate columns,
  singletons, full power sets, middle layers, and 120 seeded random pairs, and
  compared fixed-order \(Q_\pi\) and \(C_{+,\pi}\) against induced block sums
  for every global order plus the exact ordered-defect product: worst gaps
  \(1.78\times10^{-15}\), \(8.88\times10^{-16}\), \(1.15\times10^{-14}\), zero
  exact failures. `audit_square_direct.py` removed the factored-fiber objection
  by materializing the 2,025-row square as plain 14-bit masks and running the
  generic evaluators for five declared global orders in 868.52 s: worst gaps
  \(8.9\times10^{-16}\) and \(3.6\times10^{-15}\) across 3.0--4.9 million
  states per order, with three distinct order-dependent magnitudes.
* **Shared algebra machine-checked.** sympy now verifies unit transition mass,
  the reduction of each step to \(\text{const}+sD+h(s)\), shift invariance of
  \(D\), the closed form \(\max_s[sD+h(s)]=\log_2(1+2^D)\) at
  \(s=1/(1+2^{-D})\), and strict concavity via
  \(1/(s(s-1)\ln2)<0\). Both certificates use exactly that closed form.
* **Recomputation and censuses.** Independent 80-digit decimal arithmetic
  reproduced the certified \(n=7\) ratio \(0.0362591270367489647\ldots\) and the
  \(n=8\) class maximum \(0.0343354903314427\ldots\) to \(3\times10^{-16}\).
  Smaller-\(n\) controls were rerun: complete \(n=4\) (358 families, 24.30 s),
  complete \(n=5\) resume with `new_evaluations: 0`, complete \(n=6\) sizes
  3--7 (262,585 families / 1,081 orbits, 24.68 s), and the declared \(n=6\)
  two-block classes (450 orbits, 10.38 s).
* **Suite and paper.** 24 tests pass in 12.5 s, including the new dyadic,
  symbolic-algebra, and audit-artifact contracts. The paper adds an independent
  clamp-free proposition, the two new controls, and an explicit statement that
  both certificates are verified computation rather than proof-assistant
  artifacts; it rebuilds to six pages.
* **Scope unchanged.** Still non-UC, still \(\varepsilon_\vee\to1\), still no
  claim about Frankl's conjecture. What changed is that the finite input to the
  theorem no longer depends on one library, one clamp policy, or one
  representation. Every heavy command was one low-priority single-threaded
  process; both new audits are resumable and report `new_evaluations: 0` when
  complete.

### E389-EXACT-CONTROL-AND-MOVING-SIEVE (2026-08-26) — FIXED CRT IS INSUFFICIENT; THE MOVING WINDOW DOMINATES

* **Prime-key checkpoint reproduced before new work.** A fresh
  \(m\le50,\ k\le50{,}000\) atlas rebuild is semantically identical after
  removing runtime metadata: 6,493 prime-sensitive near patterns and 2,249
  shift patterns. The independent old/new comparison again reports
  95,330+6,316=101,646 changed classifications, and its JSONL ledger is
  byte-identical with SHA-256
  `c646aa350229a1bdf664474c9b7091064129d2c333b0bc8fab33ed14924b4a45`.
  Atlas and diff SHA-256 are respectively
  `d070b23bf58f988a09ed1c29c1b2ab2ccad28bab598fff1eda8a4e8b427d2de1`
  and `0190be906b56ad643e343bd24a12a7c73a9bbb1e415741c6b972b45b9984728d`.
* **Small-prime control strengthened (PROVED).** On
  \(p^L\le m+2k<p^{L+1}\), \(s_p(m,k)\) is exactly a function of
  \(k\bmod p^L\), so finite small-prime validity is an exact union of CRT
  classes on every poly-band. A uniform sufficient family is also proved:
  if \(q=p^a>m\), \(r+m<q\), and the low right carries dominate the complete
  left carries, every \(k\equiv r\pmod q\) is \(p\)-safe. The certificate
  checks 711 band values and 523,408 unrestricted-high-part extensions.
* **Fixed CRT obstruction formalized (PROVED using Dirichlet).** Every residue
  class modulo every fixed \(M\) contains arbitrarily large \(k\) with a prime
  \(p>\sqrt{m+2k}\) in one bad-window term and hence \(s_p=-1\). No nonempty
  finite union of fixed classes can be a sufficient witness construction.
  Four explicit progressions are exact-checked.
* **Same-prime and spike control completed (PROVED).** If \(p>m\) and
  \(p^e\Vert(k+i)\) at the unique bad-window position, then
  \(s_p=-e+\#\{r\ge1:2((k+i)/p^e\bmod p^r)>p^r\}\). Odd-prime mirror lifts
  supply the compensating levels; the naive binary lift fails at
  \((m,k,p)=(1,1,2)\) and needs one extra binary digit. For odd \(p>m\)
  dividing \(x=m+2k\), the exact shift slack is the cofactor's odd-quotient
  count plus \(a(m\bmod2)+v_p(m+1)-a\). This explains 5,620/5,669 failed even
  shifts, 1,042 more than the old fatal criterion, leaving 49 small-prime-only
  cases. The artifact checks 50,360 general bad-window events and 24,809
  factored shift primes; SHA-256
  `ab9baac2fd6f36e0c069ed09e924c228086c4ae345f023c77fcdeb82a0b2752b`.
* **Exact band-CRT repair cover rejects the small-prime bottleneck.** All 5,678
  boundary-comparable failed shifts reach a valid \(p\le m+1\) tier within
  radius 64; 5,321 already satisfy it at offset zero. Only 14 first tier
  candidates are witnesses. The moving window blocks 3,702 before a later
  witness and 1,962 without any witness in the radius, through 4,759
  single-level and 1,847 compensation-deficit blockers. Artifact SHA-256:
  `265dae5f3112ce88b0e9ed1b9616e46e65ca357b740af50660032d320e4b3f8b`.
* **First \(m=27\) transition searched exactly.** The published \(m=26\)
  witness shifts to a target killed by
  \(p=5{,}048{,}891{,}644{,}633\). A segmented one-process sieve checks the
  next 100,000,000 offsets: 99,999,997 have a fatal single-level bad-window
  prime. The three survivors at offsets 24,710,064, 25,238,496, and 25,238,497
  fail complete checks at 14, 6, and 7 smaller large primes. Search SHA-256:
  `d0f9128e55426ed8c28cba5c3bc0bf959b7577b8d5545d8623e754101e77142a`.
* **One-prime repair fails again at the rare survivors.** Their 27 individual
  mirror repairs all remain within the exact factorizer bound and all 27
  expose new obstruction primes. Simultaneous small-prime plus all-blocker CRT

  `02723427575e1455497387adfbac63546690370bb96f5f97650e65b7cd3f09f8`.
* **Verification and cost.** Fresh control and CRT-cover reruns are

* **Status and pivot.** Erdős #389 remains open; no novelty claim is made.

### E389-PRIME-KEY-REPAIR (2026-08-25) — ATLAS CORRECTED; BAD-WINDOW BARRIER IS NOW THE ACTIVE TARGET

* **Classification defect fixed.** The global near/shift carry-pattern keys
  omitted the prime $p$, merging equal-looking digit positions from different
  bases. Schema 2 makes $p$ the first field in every key, serialized record,
  canonical sort, comparison, and representative invariant. The preserved
  schema-1 artifact contains concrete base collisions; the new regression uses
  $(m,k)=(4,1)$, whose base-2/base-3 carry positions agree, and proves they
  remain distinct.
* **Exact impact, every case accounted.** An independent re-scan imports no
  atlas key/serialization code. It reproduces old/new distinct counts
  **1,190→6,493** (near) and **337→2,249** (shift), with event totals unchanged
  at 96,931 and 6,688. Exactly **95,330 near + 6,316 shift = 101,646** events
  belonged to 77+15 cross-prime old groups and are listed one per JSONL line.
  `m_rows` and the complete offset screen are byte-identical pre/post. A second
  full run reproduced the changed-case ledger byte-for-byte. Canonical atlas
  SHA-256: `d070b23bf58f988a09ed1c29c1b2ab2ccad28bab598fff1eda8a4e8b427d2de1`;
  case-ledger SHA-256:
  `c646aa350229a1bdf664474c9b7091064129d2c333b0bc8fab33ed14924b4a45`.
* **Pipeline hardened beyond the named bug.** Reported projections are now
  independently checked key-for-key/count-for-count in canonical order;
  tampering one prime is rejected. Inputs are read once and hashed from the
  parsed bytes, all input/output paths must be distinct, producer writes are
  same-directory atomic replaces, and atlas artifacts pin generator/core
  hashes. Three carry-position implementations agree on 1,000 deterministic
  random cases. No cache/checkpoint/restore path exists.
* **Prime-power zone theorem (PROVED).** Every level
  $\Delta_q=\lfloor x/q\rfloor+\lfloor m/q\rfloor-2\lfloor N/q\rfloor$ is
  $-1,0,$ or $+1$. For $q>m$, $(N\bmod q)$ lies in exact bad/neutral/good
  intervals. A draft extra $+1$ branch for $q\le m$ was refuted by
  $(m,k,q)=(5,2,3)$ and removed before use. The corrected formulas yield the
  bad-window theorem: every large-prime negative level divides one of the top
  $\lceil m/2\rceil$ numerator terms, and any such prime above $\sqrt{m+2k}$
  is a sharp single-level obstruction. The exact zone certificate checks
  330,023 levels and 25,536 single-level instances on 16,000 pairs.
* **Small-prime/CRT results (PROVED + CERTIFIED FINITE).** Divisibility by
  $M_m=\prod_{p\le m}p^{\min\{a:p^a>m\}}$ kills every small-prime carry.
  Odd-$m$ CRT avoidance has $\prod_{3\le p\le m}(p-1)$ safe classes and all
  5,798 safe atlas witnesses shift. Only 3/21 failed odd shifts reach a safe
  witness within one CRT period; all 21 do below $k=50{,}000$, but one needs
  559 periods. For even $m$, the fatal prime-power lemma explains 4,578/5,669
  failed shifts.
* **Prime-aware repair discriminates the mechanism.** On 5,678 non-boundary
  failed shifts, every original obstruction set becomes nonnegative within
  radius 64. Only 492 first joint repairs are witnesses; 3,224 actual repairs
  are delayed by newly exposed primes, and 1,962 have no witness in the radius.
  Thus **86.8% of the 3,716 repairable cases exhibit new-prime interference**:
  original-prime-only local CRT repair is not the dominant mechanism.
* **Alternative construction probes.** The full zero-carry corridor exhausts
  24,014 candidates for $5\le m\le18$ and finds five witnesses (one at $m=5$,
  three at $m=6$, one at $m=7$); the odd two shift, while all even three hit
  large-prime shift obstructions. A 50,000-candidate extension for $m=8..12$
  is empty. At the first unlisted index $m=27$, two 20,000-candidate partial
  corridors controlling $p\le7$ and $p\le11$ are empty, and **every candidate
  in both families has a certified single-level large-prime bad-window
  obstruction**. Stronger small-prime control did not move the barrier.
* **Verification and cost.** All producers ran through the one-process,
  reduced-priority launcher. Representative wall times: atlas 12.21 s,
  independent diff 12.93 s, CRT/spike 7.49 s, prime-repair 7.53 s, zone 0.80 s,

### UC-GATE-B-UNBOUNDED (2026-08-25) — CARTESIAN POWERS KILL EVERY SCALAR DEFECT COEFFICIENT

* **Gate B resolved in its authoritative scope.** With the existing definitions
  of $A_+$, ordered-pair defect $\varepsilon_\vee$, cap $2/5$, Reimer
  incidence, and fixed $\alpha=356069/10^7$, one has
  $$
  \boxed{c_{\rm cl}^\star=
  \sup_{\substack{\mathcal F\ {\rm cap/Reimer}\\A_+<0}}
  \frac{-A_+}{\varepsilon_\vee}=+\infty}.
  $$
  No small-defect restriction occurs in this supremum. The earlier phrase
  “near-UC search” was a proposed discovery method, not an admissibility
  condition.
* **Certified base.** The prior normalized 45-row $n=7$ family $\mathcal B$
  was independently reproduced: every coordinate count is 18, incidence is
  126, the exact Reimer threshold is 124, the exact defect is $64/81$, and a
  standalone 256-bit Arb checker proves
  $$
  A_+(\mathcal B)
  \le-0.0286491867944683178160\ldots<-\frac7{250}.
  $$
  The original certificate replay took 307.75 s and the complete declared
  $n=7$ census replay took 241.60 s, reproducing all 2,980 orbits and the
  previous 16/4 negative counts.
* **Exact lifting construction.** Put
  $\mathcal F_k=\mathcal B^{\boxtimes k}$ on disjoint seven-coordinate blocks.
  For every fixed global order, iid conditional laws split by block. A backward
  Bellman induction shows that the other block contributes the same constant
  to every child, hence cancels from the slope and action maximization. Thus
  $$
  A_+(\mathcal F_k)=kA_+(\mathcal B),\qquad
  \varepsilon_\vee(\mathcal F_k)
  =1-\left(\frac{17}{81}\right)^k.
  $$
  Therefore
  $$
  \frac{-A_+(\mathcal F_k)}{\varepsilon_\vee(\mathcal F_k)}
  >\frac{7k}{250}\to+\infty.
  $$
* **Every power remains admissible and normalized.** It has dimension $7k$,
  size $45^k$, coordinate count
  $18\cdot45^{k-1}=(2/5)45^k$, and incidence
  $126k45^{k-1}$. The exact integer witness
  $45^5=184{,}528{,}125<2^{28}=268{,}435{,}456$ proves strict Reimer
  admissibility for all $k$. Active/separating columns are inherited within
  blocks and remain distinct across independently varying blocks.
* **Adversarial square checks.** The authoritative v3 standalone verifier
  constructs the 2,025-row square, counts 3,920,000 missing joins and defect
  $6272/6561$, and evaluates the actual square Bellman DP for consecutive and
  alternating 14-coordinate orders. Both agree with twice the base-order
  optimum inside Arb residual balls of radius below $1.4\times10^{-70}$. The
  alternating control visits 2,985,831 states and encounters 405,641 ambiguous
  clamps; retaining the unconstrained concave maximum preserves a rigorous
  upper certificate. The all-order result rests on the exact induction, not
  two sampled orders.
* **New complete finite control.** The checkpointed $n=8$
  $S_1\times S_7$ census exhausts all $2^{16}-1=65{,}535$ masks. Exact filters
  retain 136 canonical families, 130 normalized; two have $A_+<0$. The largest
  COMPLETE NUMERICAL ratio is $0.034335490331443\ldots$ at a normalized
  70-row family with eight counts 28 and exact defect $283/350$, below the
  certified $n=7$ base. Runtime was 18.72 s.
* **Independent and clean verification.** The standalone checker imports no
  other `math/uc` implementation. The final v3 in-repo run took 36.68 s. A
  clean Python 3.9 virtual environment installed the frozen
  `python-flint==0.6.0` arm64 wheel and reproduced
  `PROVED_GATE_B_UNBOUNDED` after review in 59.91 s. The default Python 3.14
  source install failed because that frozen release is incompatible with
  current Cython; an initial Python 3.9 replay then exposed and prompted a
  local `popcount` compatibility fix. Both failures are preserved in

* **Tests and paper.** Ten adversarial tests cover exact thresholds,
  constraints, product defects, hand and deterministic-random product
  additivity, independent Arb/float evaluator agreement, all 40,320 $n=8$
  orders under the block quotient, full-$S_8$ canonicalization, the v3

### E389-KICKOFF (2026-08-25) — EXACT VERIFIER, ODD-SHIFT LEMMA, AND $m\le50$ ATLAS

* **Target opened.** `e389/` is now the canonical tree for Erdős Problem #389:
  prove or disprove that every $m\ge0$ has a $k\ge1$ with
  $\binom{m+k}{m}\mid\binom{m+2k}{k}$. The universal statement remains open.
* **Exact framework.** Legendre valuations and Kummer carries give
  $s_p=v_p\binom{m+2k}{k}-v_p\binom{m+k}{m}\ge0$. For the natural shift
  $(m,k)\mapsto(m+1,k-1)$,
  $s'_p=s_p+v_p(m+1)-v_p(m+2k)$. A direct floor-sum proof shows that, for odd
  $m$, every obstructing prime satisfies $p\le m$. The valid witness
  $(3,987)$ and its shifted failure at exactly $p=3$ are reproduced.
* **Published witnesses verified.** All 27 OEIS A375071 rows through $m=26$
  pass both the finite-factor valuation checker and the Kummer checker.
  Two thousand deterministic random cases also agree with direct binomial
  divisibility. Every nontrivial listed witness has minimum slack zero and is
  tight at 2--39 primes. Exact bounded scans independently reproduce the
  minima $k=207$ for $m=3$ and $k=2475$ for $m=5$; minimality of the other
  imported rows remains attributed to OEIS.
* **Complete bounded atlas.** The single-process recurrence exhausted
  $1\le m\le50$, $1\le k\le50{,}000$: 12,243 witnesses (all at $m\le10$) and
  96,931 one-prime near-witnesses. It reproduces every published minimum that
  lies under the bound ($m=1,\ldots,10$). Of 12,243 eligible natural shifts,
  6,553 succeed and 5,690 fail. Odd $m$: 6,005/6,026 succeed and all 21
  failures obey $p\le m$. Even $m$: 548/6,217 succeed, with 6,667 prime
  obstructions across 5,669 failed pairs.
* **Fixed-offset diagnosis.** Testing every $k'=k-1+\delta$ with
  $|\delta|\le16$ leaves 4,104 of 5,687 boundary-comparable natural-shift
  failures unrepaired; all tested nonzero offsets together repair only 1,583.
  This is an exact result for the declared rectangle and refutes only that
  small literal-offset menu. It is not a CRT screen or a propagation no-go.
* **Evidence and cost.** `e389/data/known_witness_verification.json` records
  the witness checks (0.57 s wall); `e389/data/atlas_m1_50_k50000.json` records
  the atlas (11.05 process-CPU s, 11.06 s wall, one reduced-priority process).
  Fourteen focused tests pass in 0.74 s. Numerical thread pools were pinned to
  one by `e389/run_low_cpu.sh`.
* **Source boundary and next action.** OEIS, the Erdős Problems record, the
  FormalConjectures statement, and the Ulas--Schinzel bibliographic record were
  inspected. The full 2013 article was not acquired, so detailed claims from
  it are not treated as verified here. Next: derive prime-dependent residue
  conditions for small-prime carry repair, then couple them by CRT while
  separately controlling new prime-power spikes in $m+2k$; do not spend the
  next pass extending the failed fixed-offset screen.

### R55 (2026-08-24c) — FORCED R(3,3) SUPPORT CUT: 6,627 / 705 D5 ORBITS

* **New exact Ramsey filter.** For each fixed `C5` edge, take the unique
  disjoint fixed nonedge. Every transposition orbit whose support contains the
  edge and avoids the nonedge contributes two vertices to their common
  intersection. Three such orbits give six vertices; `R(3,3)=6` forces a
  triangle extending the edge to `K5` or an independent triple extending the
  nonedge to `I5`. Hence every Ramsey-good completion has window multiplicity
  at most two.
* **Exact frontier movement.** Filtering all 844 certified balanced-support
  representatives rejects **139 D5 orbits / 1,245 labelled supports** and
  leaves **705 D5 orbits / 6,627 labelled supports**. Survivor orbit sizes are

### QEC (2026-08-24) — TWO PUBLISHED $[[234,8,18]]$ CODES EXACTIFIED; FIXED-POINT CLOSURE THROUGH $n=234$

* **Prior art repaired.** Liang--Liu--Song--Chen
  (arXiv:2503.03827v3, Table III) already contains two exact

  finds one map per row into $\mathbb Z_{39}\times\mathbb Z_3$; explicit qubit
  permutations verify both $H_X$ and $H_Z$ rowspace equality with the two
  open EXP-066 representatives. The codes are prior art; only the independent
  certificates and screen closure are local.
* **Two-root exact theorem.** A minimum nontrivial kernel word has no proper
  zero-syndrome subset: a logical subset is a lighter logical, and removing a
  stabilizer subset leaves a lighter representative. Thus connected-cluster
  recursion reaches the word from its least coordinate. Translation plus an
  exact block-column swap reduces each 234-column lower proof to two rooted
  searches.
* **Eight exact runs.** For both representatives, original and block-swapped
  searches exhaust weights 1--16 and replay. Initial walls are
  1,953.28/1,961.95 s and 2,051.99/2,075.03 s; replays are

  Even kernel weight excludes 17; independent NumPy/bitset checks verify

  dist-m4ri coordinator can declare the one-root round complete after claiming
  the column but before incrementing its active-worker count. Every affected
  run was cancelled. EXP-067 pins the source-archived race-free legacy
  `STANDALONE` entrypoint and rejects the modern three-integer output
  (FR-033). The native binary remains trusted code; no proof trace is claimed.
* **Global promotion.** The literature battery is now 29 sourced / 27

  reference rebinding rechecks every carried witness. Across 22 odd-lattice
  frontiers / 4,862 classes / 150,581 represented pairs, all 4,658 referenced
  classes are dominated; 204 high-$k$ classes remain `no_reference`; zero

* **Global fail-open repair.** Final review found that v10 had validated

### R55 (2026-08-24b) — BALANCED-SUPPORT CENSUS: 7,872 / 844 D5 ORBITS

* **Exact Walsh reduction.** For the `f=5` involution branch, write `x_E` for
  even-support multiplicities and complement the odd supports to even
  multiplicities `z_T`. Replication ten and pair multiplicity five determine
  every Fourier coefficient on the sixteen even subsets and give
  `z_T=(5-A_T)/2`, where `A_T` sums `x_E` over
  `|E xor T|∈{0,4}`. Validity is exactly `A_T∈{1,3,5}`. The transform is
  involutive, so all valid multiplicities lie in `{0,1,2}`.
* **Complete census.** The producer exhausts 996,216 ternary vectors, reduced
  from `C(25,15)=3,268,760` raw even multisets, and finds exactly **7,872**
  balanced, moment-feasible support multisets on a labelled fixed `C5`. The
  explicit `Aut(C5)=D5` action gives exactly **844** orbits, with size
  histogram `1:2, 5:110, 10:732`. Burnside reproduces 844 from fixed counts
  `7872`, `2` per nonidentity rotation, and `112` per reflection. These counts
  are upper bounds on support types realizable by an SRG completion.
* **Disjoint coverage checker.** The checker imports no producer symbol and
  does not assume the ternary bound. It constructs and rationally inverts the
  odd `16×16` incidence-moment matrix, recursively covers every weak
  composition of ten even blocks, and independently generates `D5` by
  filtering all 120 point permutations. It reconstructs the same 7,872
  balanced designs, all 844 representatives, orbit sizes, Burnside counts,
  and domain-separated hashes.
* **Sharper signed frontier.** With `R_vi=1-2N_vi`, the remaining invariant
  and anti-invariant Seidel blocks satisfy
  `Q_F R+RW=-J`, `W²+2RᵀR=45I-2J`, `T²=45I`, and
  `W_ij²+T_ij²=4`. Thus the next gate is an exact signed completion over 844
  balanced-support candidates, followed by orbit-level `K5/I5` constraints.
* **Source-verified positive controls.** The 1,470,604-byte published `S3`
  payload (SHA-256
  `a53366d919f5d29876d07031a65c1bd722d05e88b59b39a04e9988c9162ebec0`)
  contains 288 labelled SRGs with the specified fixed-five involution. All
  288 satisfy the support and signed equations and contain both `K5` and `I5`.
  A hash-bound dependency check proves that their 26 support types occur among
  the 844 balanced candidates. They validate the model but are neither a
  complete SRG census nor Ramsey-good witnesses.
* **Solver probes stayed exploratory.** A 1,028,730-clause involution CNF
  accepts a published completion under its exact 380 relation assumptions
  with zero conflicts. Removing those assumptions did not terminate within
  the probe limits; the symmetric rotation support also returned UNKNOWN
  after 200,001 conflicts. No SAT/SMT verdict is used in the theorem.
* **Current certificate route.** CP-2026 VeriPB/CakePB projected enumeration
  can certify preserved-variable counts, while LeanSMS checks individual
  symmetry witnesses before LRAT. Neither tool makes a preserved projection
  an orbit quotient automatically. This census therefore keeps its
  solver-free explicit group action; any signed-completion solver must use
  VeriPB dominance or separately checked symmetry lemmas.
* **Evidence.** New files:
  `src/involution_f5_support_census.py`,
  `src/check_involution_f5_support_census.py`,
  `tests/test_involution_f5_support_census.py`, and
  `data/involution_f5_support_census.json` (schema 1). The published-data
  validator and artifact now check the involution positive controls.
* **Artifact commitments.** The 503,066-byte balanced-census artifact has
  SHA-256
  `520e2cb453cc2efee7925636af018316acca3e375a8107886c5e9c90c3727aaa`;
  the 1,735-byte published-control artifact has SHA-256
  `86b92f96ba72afdbdd8f6375b3c8c6bf8d1d3c2b7afe35b5f9fbdc7a63d5539f`.
* **Verification.** The focused census/control suite passed **25/25** in

### ISING3D WAVE 24 (2026-08-24) — ALL-EVEN-STAR SIGN LAW; BOUNDED EXCEPTION SCHEMES; SELECTED CALLEN BLOCK

* **Graph-uniform spectral algebra.** Every connected simple nonpath `n`-vertex, `m`-edge
  layer has at most `[2^n(2n+2m)]^(n+2)` full-spectrum exceptional couplings; uniformly at
  fixed `n`, at most `[2^n(n^2+n)]^(n+2)`. The proof uses a degree-`2n+2m`
  polynomial transfer representative, wave-22 complex finiteness, and the 2025
  ambient-dimension projective degree theorem. Paths are excluded; this is not emptiness.
* **All-even-star theorem and local Gaussian structure.** For every even star and `K!=0`,
  each nonempty log-Walsh sector of size `2r` has strict sign `(-1)^(r+1)`.
  At `0<v=tanh(K)<1`, the degree-six weight is a pure-even matchgate; all `4096`
  identities vanish exactly. Ordinary CP rank and every flattening rank are two, but
  Walsh-basis nonnegative all-leg CP rank is exactly `32`. The normalized checkerboard
  contraction is the positive cycle-space polynomial, not a global nonplanar Pfaffian theorem.
* **Non-termwise Callen result.** At `0<v=tanh(K)<1`, the fixed contact-free
  selected-pivot/far-mark proper-even/odd leakage matrix has rank
  `2^(d-1)-C(d,d/2)/2`; every leakage-canceling combination has zero pair coefficient.
  Cubic inversion yields six exact higher-template identities in a `19x16` rank-13 block,
  but deleting the pair row lowers rank to 12.
* **Verification and review.** Producers `e244/e245/e246` passed `13/13`, `9/9`, and
  `10/10`; all three clean-room verifiers passed. Adversarial theorem reviews found and
  drove repairs for retained-spin normalization, path scope, dependency/resource provenance,
  external-source registration, and semantic false-pass gates; final rereviews returned PASS.
  The tree now has 166 tests.
  No full suite ran under a nine-user host load near 98.

  `b716c320aa6e253dc26345497ff512ff94949fd2ae5cc5c3bff3666ce41f7608`.
  Snapshot docs predate this self-reference.

### R55 (2026-08-24) — INVOLUTION FIXED COUNTS REDUCED TO 1,5,9,13

* **Exact SRG-only theorem.** Every nonidentity involution of an
  `srg(45,22,10,11)` fixes only `1`, `5`, `9`, or `13` vertices. No Ramsey
  hypothesis enters this reduction. It strengthens the source-verified
  Behbahani--Lam general bound `f≤25` and the elementary `f≤21` row bound by
  eliminating `f=17,21`.
* **Anti-invariant matrix method.** For `c=(45-f)/2` transposition orbits,
  adjacency on `e_x-e_τ(x)` is a symmetric integral matrix
  `C` with `C²+C=11I`, diagonal values `-1,0`, and off-diagonal values
  `-1,0,1`. Conjugate spectral multiplicities give `c` even and exactly `c/2`
  edge-pair orbits. Every row has exactly eleven nonzero off-diagonal entries,
  initially leaving `f∈{1,5,9,13,17,21}`.
* **The two high-fixed cases close by parity.** At `c=12`, switching one
  complete-support row positive makes an edge-class inner product demand that
  ten signs sum to one. At `c=14`, the zero graph is 2-regular on two
  seven-vertex diagonal parts. Mod-two cross degree reduces it to exactly
  twelve internal/alternating cycle shapes. Their common-support parity
  violations number `14,24,24,34,22,28,34,42,28,32,28,36`; every case is
  impossible before signs are assigned.
* **Redundant exact exhaustion.** A canonical positive spanning-tree gauge
  selects one representative of every switching class. The producer checks
  **10,008,588** `c=14` row assignments and **21,936** surviving prefixes
  across all twelve shapes, finding no matrix. Search hashes now bind a
  versioned domain containing the order, diagonal assignment, zero edges,
  gauge, and prefix serialization; all twelve hashes are distinct.
* **Sharp open interface.** Fixed supports satisfy
  `H²+H+2XXᵀ=11(J+I)`. At `f=5`, all `2^10` labelled fixed graphs reduce to
  twelve labelled copies of `C5`, but an explicit 20-column design survives:
  replication ten and pair multiplicity five. Thus `f=5` has reached signed
  incidence completion. Cases `f=9,13` still require fixed-graph/support work;
  no global “signed completion only” claim is made for them.
* **Evidence and hardening.** New files are
  `src/involution_srg_frontier.py`,
  `src/check_involution_srg_frontier.py`,
  `tests/test_involution_srg_frontier.py`, and
  `data/involution_srg_frontier.json` (schema 1). The checker imports no
  producer symbol and independently generates the twelve shapes. Review found
  three P2 integrity gaps—JSON type confusion/duplicate keys, mutable cached
  certificate trees, and trace-only search hashes. All were fixed with strict
  parsing and exact-type comparison, immutable canonical-JSON caches, and
  domain-separated commitments; regressions cover every escape. Final
  mathematical review and code rereview both returned **ACCEPTED**, with no
  remaining P0/P1/P2.
* **Verification.** The focused suite passed **19/19** in **60.673 s**. The
  hardened combined involution, odd-prime, order-three, published-data, and
  Cayley suites passed **76/76** in **486.498 s**. The standalone checker,
  `py_compile`, artifact parse, and campaign-spec parse all exited zero.
* **Scope.** Fixed counts `1,5,9,13` remain open in the Ramsey-good conference
  lane. The theorem says nothing about non-strongly-regular
  Ramsey(5,5,45) graphs and changes no Ramsey-number bound. The next exact
  target is the `f=5` signed incidence completion, with VeriPB-certified
  non-local gluing and asymmetric complementary SAT remaining the global
  alternatives.
