  upper bound: \(K(12)\) remains open.

### KOBON (2026-08-24c) — ENDPOINT CLOSURE + MULTIPOINT-INCIDENCE FRONT

* **New endpoint-closure theorem.** For faces \(\{r,a,b\}\) and
  \(\{r,c,d\}\), if \(P_{ra}=P_{rc}\) and the remote vertices
  \(P_{rb},P_{rd}\) leave that point on the same ray of \(r\), then the remote
  vertices coincide. If both endpoints coincide, the faces share an
  arrangement edge and their apexes lie on opposite sides. This strictly
  extends the prior shared-line-pair lemma to faces sharing only one line.
* **New perturbation theorem.** Selected faces with only simple vertices
  survive one sufficiently small generic perturbation: all their direct-gap
  inequalities are strict, and new crossings of formerly parallel lines flee
  to infinity. Hence at most \(\bar a_3^s(n)\) selected faces avoid finite
  multipoints. Since \(\bar a_3^s(12)=37\), every target-39 family has at least
  two multipoint-incident faces.
* **Exact falsification passed.** The expanded rational audit still covers
  3,600 arrangements / 27,705 faces. New coverage is 73,349 pairs of faces
  sharing exactly one line, 1,607 endpoint collisions, 84 colliding same-ray
  cases, 42 shared segments, and 1,591 shared-pair segments; zero violations.
  Fixed exact \(K(10)=25\) and \(K(12)\ge38\) geometries satisfy the combined
  reified-face, endpoint, perturbation, \(K(4)\), and hereditary CNF exactly;
  the \(n=12\) witness has eight multipoint-incident faces.
* **Implementation.** `--endpoint-closure` adds 154,440 clauses and no
  variables over the shared-ray \(n=12\) lane. `--simple-bound 37` adds 656
  variables / 7,036 clauses and forces two incident faces.
  `--k4-bound` adds 1,980 ternary clauses and no variables. The resulting
  11-/10-deletion instance is 375,037 variables / 1,005,366 clauses, SHA-256
  `6dca104aa120ee1f209aada8aca07a08bf3341f1335e6e99f4ae1f11bc2ba76b`.
  Adding checked \(K(9)=21\) deletion cuts gives 842,317 / 1,917,926,
  SHA-256
  `34406004c932ce77d33f76f8b7d19c7b0f5a2e3e241e94651846269bf750f2df`.
* **Controlled performance is deliberately mixed.** Fresh CaDiCaL
  \((8,16)\) runs were 249,513 conflicts / 25.20 s for shared-ray, 221,905 /
  18.17 s for endpoint closure, and 178,920 / 19.09 s for all local cuts.
  At \((9,22)\), endpoint closure worsened 958,507 / 138.38 s to 1,020,652 /
  169.56 s; all local cuts were slower again. SAT controls \((8,15)\) and
  \((9,21)\) remained SAT.
* **High-risk negative results retained.** Exact face reification was fast at
  \((8,16)\) but extremely slow at both \(n=9\) controls. A zero-aware
  rank-three Grassmann--Plücker formulation matched 195,000 exact determinant
  signs and 1,386,000 relations, but found no semantic gap through \(n=7\) and
  doubled the \(n=8\) endpoint runtime. Neither is in the new live lanes.
* **Independent review hardened the boundary.** No P1 soundness defect was
  found. Three P2 issues were fixed: reified-face heredity now emits the
  direct subset cap instead of the exact-target complement, the open
  \(n=12\) witness is called a 38-face record rather than an optimum, and the
  endpoint theorem explicitly assumes two distinct faces. The focused suite
  remains 36/36 green after the fixes.
* **Recent-source survey.** Savchuk (arXiv:2507.07951), Rote
  (arXiv:2503.02336), Parpalak--Utkin (arXiv:2604.22035 and 2607.29236), and
  Radtke--Keszegh--Lauff (arXiv:2601.20574) were read at source. The newest
  exhaustive search is explicitly odd/simple/pseudoline; it leaves the even,
  non-simple frontier here outside scope. Its defect/wiring methods therefore
  informed experiments but were not imported as universal clauses.
* **Two bold lanes live, no verdict.** `n12-gap-endpoint-mi-k4-t39` runs
  Kissat seed 0; `n12-gap-endpoint-sub9-t39` runs seed 1. Neither logs a proof.
  UNSAT would require a proof-producing rerun and independent checking; SAT
  would require exact rational realization. \(K(12)\) remains open.

### KOBON (2026-08-24b) — SHARED-RAY ENDPOINT LEMMA; OPT-IN N12 LANE LIVE

* **New local theorem.** If two triangular arrangement faces share lines
  \(i,j\) and their remote vertices on shared line \(i\) lie on the same ray
  from \(P_{ij}\), those vertices coincide: otherwise the nearer vertex lies
  in the relative interior of the farther face side. Thus \(i\) and the two
  third lines are concurrent. The same holds on \(j\). In a no-concurrency
  arrangement, two faces using one line pair therefore occupy opposite
  sectors.
* **Opt-in encoding, not a default regression.**
  `add_selected_face_sector_bounds(..., shared_ray=True)` emits the four
  five-literal endpoint-concurrency clauses for each pair of faces sharing two
  lines. `gap_faces.py --shared-ray-bounds` exposes them and implies the prior
  sector cuts. The existing `--sector-bounds` path remains byte-identical.
  At \(n=12,T=39\) the new lane adds 11,880 clauses and no variables:
  374,381 variables / 841,910 clauses, SHA-256
  `0aa9e81e1806d5ff7413669d7357454c9e08a496d2172d8b4a512b27115b7e7d`.
  `n12-gap-shared-ray-t39` is live at nice 19 with no verdict and no proof
  logging.
* **Exact falsification and sharpness.** The rational replay covered 3,600
  arrangements, 27,705 faces, 83,115 face-sector assignments, 17,325
  shared-pair face pairs and 1,591 same-ray cases: every same-ray case had the
  required remote concurrency, with zero violations. A six-line exact control
  realizes four faces on one pair, one in each sector, so the four-sector bound
  remains sharp.
* **Performance is mixed, so the cut stays optional.** On same-snapshot
  CaDiCaL controls it improved \((8,16)\) from 164,869 to 153,108 conflicts
  but was neutral at \((9,22)\) (203,892 to 206,959). Kissat seed 0 worsened
  \((9,22)\) from 228,022 to 300,407 conflicts. SAT controls at \((8,15)\)
  and \((9,21)\) remained SAT; no general speedup is claimed.
* **Verification and review.** The focused suite is 24/24 green. Independent
  code review found no P1 defect and identified one P2 test gap: positive
  remote concurrency and one asymmetric opposite-ray case were not asserted
  SAT. Both escapes were added and pass. Fable review was attempted but the
  local CLI hit its seven-day model limit before inference. Hash-bound record:
  `scratch/kobon/shared_ray_bound_experiment.json`.

### H10/Q (2026-08-24g) — L34 SHARP AP1 PAIR-MOMENT THRESHOLD

* **Exact combinatorial criterion — PROVED.**  Even Hilbert parity gives
  \[
  \mathbf1_{\{R_{\rm bad}=0\}}
  \ge1-\binom{R_{\rm bad}}2,
  \qquad
  \mathcal N_{\rm good}
  \ge\#\mathcal A_z-\mathcal P_2^{\rm root}.
  \]
  Here the root moment oversieves even valuations.  A strict relative
  root-pair bound below \(1\) proves AP1 without exact odd valuations;
  the formerly displayed \(o\)-bound is stronger than necessary.
* **Unrestricted envelope and threshold — exact.**  For octic size
  \(X^8\), \(\mu(\vartheta)=\frac12\log(8/\vartheta)\), and the
  unrestricted pair envelope \(\mu(\vartheta)^2/2<1\) iff
  \(\vartheta>8e^{-2\sqrt2}=0.4728459724\ldots\).  The actual
  divisor-product constraint only shrinks the pair region.  At L23's
  \(\vartheta=0.49\), the envelope is \(0.9749604961\ldots\), leaving a
  safe \(2.5\%\) margin.
* **Exact open estimate.**  A sharp conditioned bound
  \[
  \mathcal P_2^{\rm root}(X)
  \le(0.9749604961\ldots+o(1))\#\mathcal A_z(X)
  \]
  would imply AP1, intermediate H, and the six-variable theorem without
  classical Schinzel H.  It is not proved: root-pair moduli exceed
  \(X^{0.98}\), and the semilinear sieve at ratio \(50/49\) does not
  give the needed sharp prime-weighted constant after small-prime
  conditioning (`math/h10q/l34_ap1_pair_threshold.py`;
  `math/h10q/data/l34_ap1_pair_threshold.jsonl`;
  `math/h10q/THEOREMS.md`, L34).

### H10/Q (2026-08-24f) — L33 UNWEIGHTED TWO-LARGE PAIR MAIN

* **Positive unsigned main — PROVED.**  For any fixed
  \(0<\alpha<\beta<1/2\), let \(\mathcal U_2\) count unweighted
  \(t\in(X,2X]\) in simultaneous bad-root classes for
  \(p,q\in[X^\alpha,X^\beta]\).  L23 Chebotarev and elementary CRT
  counting give
  \[
  \mathcal U_2(X;\alpha,\beta)
  \sim\frac18\log^2(\beta/\alpha)X.
  \]
  The \(O(1)\)-per-class error totals \(O(X^{2\beta})=o(X)\).
* **Beyond-BV consequence.**  With \(\alpha=0.49\) and
  \(0.49<\beta<1/2\), every pair modulus is at least \(X^{0.98}\).
  Thus the positive unsigned pair main in the first unavailable range
  is a theorem, not only a formal local-density warning.
* **Exact remaining transfer.**  The result is unweighted and uses the
  mod-\(p\) root oversieve.  Replacing class counts by
  \(\Lambda(Q(t))\), enforcing \(v_p(G)=v_q(G)=1\) through \(p^2q^2\),
  and conditioning on small-prime cleanliness remain open.  The new
  producer passed `py_compile` and its exact CRT replay
  (`math/h10q/l33_unweighted_pair_main.py`;
  `math/h10q/data/l33_unweighted_pair_main.jsonl`;
  `math/h10q/THEOREMS.md`, L33).

### H10/Q (2026-08-24e) — L32 AUTOMATIC-\(\Phi\) TARGET MAPS

* **Dyadic base parameterization — PROVED.**  For every rational
  parameter,
  \[
  \sigma(t)=\frac{t}{1+2t^2},\quad
  a(t)=1+2\sigma(t),\quad
  b_\kappa(u)=\frac{u^2+\kappa}{5u^2+\kappa},
  \quad\kappa\in\{1,2,-2\},
  \]
  satisfy \(v_2(\sigma)\ge0\) and
  \(v_2(a)=v_2(b_\kappa)=0\).  This is a complete valuation proof.
* **Every regular odd target occurs in the same finite menu — PROVED.**
  The squareclasses of \(A\) and \(A+4\) along \(a(t)\) are represented
  by quartics \(F_5,F_9\) with resultant \(2^{28}\).  Weil gives
  \[
  N_w\ge\frac{w-13\sqrt w}{4}-4>0\qquad(w\ge211),
  \]
  and exact exhaustion closes \(5\le w<211\).  Separately,
  \((-1|w)(-2|w)(2|w)=1\) selects a \(b_\kappa\)-numerator with a
  simple root and hence a lift satisfying \(v_w(b_\kappa)=1\).
  L30 then supplies local \(H_2\)-points at both \(2\) and \(w\).
* **Two global-section menus closed.**  The full proportional-trace
  ansatz \((b-1)^2/b=4ra^2\) has branches \(b=t^2/r,r/t^2\).
  A unit-\(a\), valuation-one target forces \(v_w(r)=-1\), so every
  finite fixed \(r\)-menu misses almost every target.  The identities
  \(b=1\pm2a\Rightarrow N_g=\pm64a^5\) are exact, but target regularity
  restricts them to \(w\equiv5,19\bmod24\).
* **Global status unchanged.**  The rational maps introduce new
  numerator and denominator primes whose Hilbert symbols are not
  controlled.  No global cube-compatible \(H_2\)-root, fixed-family
  detector asymptotic, AP1, or six-variable Schinzel-H removal follows.
  The new producer passed `py_compile` and generated six exact rows in
  \(12.461\) s, including \(667\) odd-prime target cross-checks below
  \(5000\) and \(167\) \(b\)-menu checks below \(1000\)
  (`math/h10q/l32_local_parameter_frontier.py`;

### H10/Q (2026-08-24d) — L31 EXACT RECIPROCAL MEMBER; CUBE/DISPERSION/AP1 WALLS

* **First exact fixed-field reciprocal member — PROVED.**  On the
  mandatory slice take
  \[
  (w,a,z,Z,q_0,b,\rho,\lambda)
  =(13,3,13,169,1,13,14/13,12).
  \]
  The norm value is
  \[
  M=\frac{2^4Q}{3^2 37^3 83^2 1033^2},\qquad
  Q=14082426920623718389.
  \]
  A recursive Pocklington chain certifies \(Q\), and the complete table
  gives \((M,26)_v=+1\) at
  \(2,3,13,37,83,1033,Q,\infty\).  Hasse--Minkowski proves the norm
  point.  The lift \(\lambda^2=13(13\rho^2-4)=144\) is explicit, so the
  trace-base circularity guard is satisfied.
* **Quartic near miss — exact, but outside the cube pullback.**  The
  unique finite constant-\(c\) bridge square occurs at \(a=1\),
  \(c=-64/25\).  It gives
  \[
  b=\frac{2101}{25000}
  -\frac{2\lambda^2}{(\lambda^2-5)^2}.
  \]
  At \(\lambda=3\),
  \[
  b=-3253/3125,\qquad Z=2033125/6101423
  \]
  satisfies every bridge guard, \(\Phi\) at \(2\), and \(H_2(9)=0\).
  But \(Z=5^4\cdot3253/(1009\cdot6047)\) is not a cube, and at
  \(3253\) the bridge value \(c\) is a unit.  Imposing \(Z=z^3\)
  reduces to two explicit squarefree genus-\(2\) curves.  Magma
  V2.29-9 `RationalPointsGenus2` returns only \(z=0\) on each with
  completeness `true`, closing this unique constant-\(c\) section.
  No controlled point on another section is proved.
* **Dispersion target corrected.**  The bad-root pair classes are exact
  CRT products beginning at modulus \(X^{0.98}>D_{\rm BV}\); exact odd
  valuation uses \(p^2q^2\).  Character expansion leaves the positive
  \(R_pR_q/4\) term, and the reciprocal-prime sum over
  \([X^{0.49},X^8]\) is constant-order.  Therefore signed cancellation
  alone cannot discard the unsigned two-large sector.  The natural
  missing theorem is a pointwise fixed-family Buchstab or analytic
  Hilbert-detector asymptotic with positive zero-bad main term.
* **Exact Schinzel replacement isolated.**  AP1 asks for one selected
  aligned class per cell with \(Q(t)\) prime and \(R_{\rm bad}\le1\).
  Hilbert reciprocity makes \(R_{\rm bad}\) even, so AP1 is equivalent
  to intermediate H inside the L19--L22 protocol.  L23 permits
  \(0,2,\ldots,16\), hence does not prove AP1.
* **Scope and verification.**  The \(w=13\) row is one member, not a
  uniform theorem.  The cube-compatible quartic point, fixed-family
  detector asymptotic, AP1, removal of classical Schinzel H, and every
  unconditional count change remain OPEN.  The final L31 producer and
  `py_compile` passed in \(0.315\) s with exact primality, factor, lift,
  CRT, parity, and Hilbert checks.  The external Magma request returned
  complete point sets for both genus-\(2\) curves.  The default and
  extended suites passed in \(58.56\) s and \(179.74\) s.  Independent
  final review returned PASS after correcting the dispersion counting
  scales, finite modulus cutoff, AP1 protocol scope, and stale
  single-member wording.  LSP diagnostics were unavailable because no
  Python language server is installed.  Final TeX logs have no
  undefined controls, references, citations, or fatal errors.  Main:
  \(35\) pages / \(532{,}634\) bytes, SHA-256
  `836b1c56fb4d00688f2086fbdcf469f88aef10115a80883caffb94b3b63832db`.
  Companion: \(12\) pages / \(287{,}629\) bytes, SHA-256
  `28be04011b3e52fbd2dadc0b6c471d5b328866a034edab94db3d0ee1428f9a01`.
  The exact external Magma request/output and intrinsic-help semantics

* **Coupled-cover degree drop — PROVED.**  On the L24--L27 square

  This is quadratic in \(m\), hence even quartic in \(\lambda\).
  Its leading coefficient has exact \(2\)-adic valuation \(2\) on
  every \(\Phi\)-base.  The discriminant is
  \(256A^2J\), with the explicit quadratic \(J\) recorded in L30.
* **Displayed local obligations — PROVED with exact scope.**
  Normalizing \(m=1+8t\) gives an even value and unit derivative, so
  Hensel solves every dyadic stratum.  A two-quadratic character count
  supplies fibre-regular aligned rows for every \(w\ge5\).  At \(w=3\),
  the exact fibre \((a,b,z)=(5,3,3)\) has
  \(v_3(H_2(1))=3>2v_3(H_2'(1))=2\), giving
  \(m\in1+9\mathbb Z_3\); the empty control \((1,3,3)\) proves
  fixed-\(a\) nonuniformity.  One guarded real stratum works.
* **Generic escape routes — PROVED rigid.**  Every rational L11c
  square-branch tie has the same target norm field
  \(\mathbb Q(\sqrt{2b})\).  A \(b\)-adic leading-unit argument excludes
  a generic rational section for every \(d(b)\), including poles and

  its discriminant forces \(m/A\) square, impossible on \(\Phi\) when
  \(s\ne0\).  The marginal \(d=2a\) tie is not uniformly dyadic:
  \((a,b,z)=(1,-15,-9)\) has \((M_d,2b)_2=-1\).
* **Trace route — exact descent, circular shortcut blocked.**  On
  L26's square pullback,
  \[
  M=T(u)/(DA)^2,\qquad [2b]=[2(u+2)].
  \]
  The base change \(u+2=w\rho^2\) gives an irreducible degree-\(8\)

  \(\lambda^2=w(w\rho^2-4)\).  Parameterizing that conic recovers
  \(b=wq^2\), exactly L29's parity slice.
* **Latest source routing.**  Frei--Sofos arXiv:2604.07047 supplies an
  analytic Hilbert-symbol detector but only an \(L^2\) coefficient-box

### H10/Q (2026-08-24b) — L29 RECIPROCAL LIFT CLASSIFIED; DYADIC INPUT COMPRESSED

* **Complete local lift law — PROVED.**  On the L26 square-\(Z\)
  family, with \(t=v_2(Z)\in2\mathbb Z\),
  \[
  \theta^2-4\in\mathbb Q_2(\theta)^{\times2}
  \iff t=-2\ \text{or}\ t\ge2.
  \]
  The positive stratum is strong Hensel.  The \(t=0\) and \(t\le-4\)
  strata are excluded by exhaustive necessary congruences for every
  possible pair of Eisenstein quartic factors: \(2048\) candidates
  modulo \(16\) and \(524{,}288\) modulo \(64\), zero survivors.
* **Exceptional split stratum — PROVED by an ordinary resultant.**
  At \(t=-2\),
  \(\mathcal R(Y)=\operatorname{Res}_x(S,Y-(x^4+6x^3+6))\) has exact
  Newton vertices \((0,21),(4,10),(8,0)\), hence slopes
  \(-11/4,-5/2\).  Irreducibility of \(S\) would make all conjugate
  resultant roots equi-valued, a contradiction; the first polygon then
  forces \(4+4\).
* **Target-preserving compressor — PROVED.**
  \(Z=8z^2/(1+z^2)\) satisfies
  \(v_p(Z)>0\iff v_p(z)>0\) for every odd \(p\), while
  \(v_2(Z)\ge2\) for every nonzero rational \(z\).  It therefore forces
  the locally split reciprocal stratum without changing soundness,
  odd targets, or the formal count.
* **Fixed-field route — exact reduction, member OPEN.**
  \(b=w\rho^2\) turns \((M,2b)\) into \((M,2w)\), but the resulting
  conic bundle has no generic section.  Eight named fibres are exactly
  soluble.  On \((w,a,Z)=(3,5,9)\), the \(24\) decided positive odd
  \(w\)-adic units \(\rho<100\) have \(0/2/4\) bad-place counts
  \(11/12/1\); \(9\) refusals are excluded.  This is EVIDENCE and
  retains parity pairs.
* **L27 pruning — PROVED at stated scopes.**
  \(C_2\equiv128\bmod256\) excludes
  \(\lambda=\pm1,\pm A\), and \(y=0\) forces a conic with no
  \(\mathbb Q_3\)-point.  Together with the linear-\(B\) theorem, these
  are three section no-gos, not global emptiness.

  replaced after an independent objection; a second independent audit
  validated the resultant/embedding argument at confidence \(0.82\).

### H10/Q (2026-08-24) — L28 UNIFORM TRACE-FIELD CLOSURE

* **L28 — PROVED uniform local irreducibility.**  On the L26 even
  pullback, shifting \(w=u-2\) gives a depressed quartic whose dyadic
  Newton polygon has one half-integral-slope segment.  Linear factors
  are impossible.  Any quadratic factor forces a square root on the
  Ferrari resolvent; its five possible valuation cases normalize to
  \(3,2,4,4,2\bmod8\), so none exists.  Thus the trace quartic is
  irreducible over \(\mathbb Q_2\), hence over \(\mathbb Q\), for every
  canonical parameter.
* **The bad-sign extension is also uniform.**  The exact norm is
  \[
  N(2(\theta+2))=
  \frac{64\{A^3D^2+64a^8Z^4(a^4-A)^2\}}{a^8Z^4A^2}.
  \]
  The braced factor differs from \(A^3D^2\) by a relative term in
  \(2^{10}\mathbb Z_2\), so the norm has squareclass
  \(A\equiv5\bmod8\).  Hence \(2(\theta+2)\) is nonsquare and
  \(T(v^2/2-2)\) is irreducible.  TraceFieldAudit independently returned
  SOUND at confidence \(0.99\), with no finding.
* **Creative global follow-up.**  For L27, the coefficient linear in
  \(B=2b\) factors exactly as
  \[
  -16ABm(m-A)\bigl((s+1)^2m-(s-1)^2A\bigr),\qquad m=\lambda^2.
  \]
  Every cancellation factor is incompatible with the rational conic or
  leaves the full eliminant nonzero.  This kills the natural

### ISING3D WAVE 22 (2026-08-23) — ENDPOINT SPECTRA; THREE TRACE CANDIDATES; EXACT LOCAL REFORMULATIONS

* **Generic full-spectrum Gaussianity classified up to finite exceptions.** At the
  low-temperature corner, ordered transfer valuations are exactly graph cut sizes;
  the cut multiset is a Boolean subset sum iff a simple graph is a forest. At the
  high-temperature corner, the first graph-sensitive Walsh-band splitting is the
  unsigned hard-core token graph, while a full cube requires the additive compound
  pinned by the actual one-particle band; the exterior signing is balanced iff a
  connected graph is a path. Hence every connected simple nonpath graph has only
  finitely many full-cube physical couplings (Chevalley, archived tag 054K). The
  exceptional set is not generally located or proved empty (`H600`-`H608`,
  `e235`-`e236`).
* **The finite open `2x3` continuum is reduced to three exact trace candidates.**
  A specialization-stable rank-eight quotient gives a primitive squarefree dense
  `E(q)` of degree 971. It has exactly three `q>1` roots, mapping to certified
  `t` intervals `(0.397429,0.39743)`, `(0.160347,0.160348)`, and
  `(0.052285,0.052286)`. Each is a genuine seven-trace algebraic branch; whether
  its mode sextic has six roots `u_i>=4` is unresolved (`H628`-`H632`, `e238`).
* **Exact octahedral decimation.** Eliminating a degree-six star gives a unique
  octahedral log weight with `c2>0,c4<0,c6>0` for every real `K!=0`; the `c6`
  sign is an exact coefficient-positive polynomial certificate. Thus checkerboard
  elimination is exactly a 2-/4-/6-spin model and cannot close inside arbitrary
  visible fields/pairs (`H596`-`H599`, `e234`).
* **Exact Callen reconstruction, with the compression boundary proved.** The
  six-neighbour field has explicit rational `c1(v),c3(v),c5(v)`. On every finite
  loopless undirected free-boundary graph with uniform coupling and no field, the
  complete Callen system has formal rank `2^n-1` and reconstructs the unnormalised
  Gibbs moments up to scale. It uses `2^n` correlators and therefore is not a
  thermodynamic compression. Endpoint/specialization and boundary hypotheses were
  tightened after review (`H614`-`H617`, correction `H623`, `e240`).
* **Two new topology/determinant boundaries.** Uniformly averaging phase-free
  Hashimoto determinants over all graph-`H1` twists kills every shortest cycle:
  first mismatch `0` versus twice the girth-cycle count on every cyclic simple
  graph. The complete 32-class open-cube average was independently rebuilt.
  Separately, open boxes satisfy
  `gamma>=max(0,ceil((abc-ab-bc-ca+4)/4))` and are planar iff a side is one or
  they are `2x2xL`; `4^g` is recorded only as a classical construction count,
  never an arbitrary Pfaffian lower bound (`H618`-`H627`, `e239`, `e241`).
* **W-law finite mechanism survives `L=9`; easy induction does not.** All 49

  cores refute raw singleton peeling as an all-`L` proof (`H610`-`H613`, `e237`).

### R55 (2026-08-23) — ODD-PRIME AUTOMORPHISM LANE CLOSED; RAMSEY CONFERENCE AUT IS A 2-GROUP

* **EXACT STRUCTURAL THEOREM:** if a strongly regular graph with parameters
  `(45,22,10,11)` has neither a clique nor an independent set of order five,
  then its automorphism-group order is a power of two. The existing
  order-three certificate is composed, and new exact arguments exclude prime
  orders `5,7,11` and every prime at least `13`. This changes no bound on
  `R(5,5)`.
* **Order five — mixed analytic/finite closure.** Galois trace gives quotient
  trace `2c`. A quotient-diagonal moment valid for all internal degrees
  `d∈{0,2,4}` excludes `f=10,15,…,40` for every
  `srg(45,22,10,11)`, without a Ramsey hypothesis:
  `Σt=22−d−s`, `Σt²=66−d−d²−5s`; at `c=7` the three Cauchy-gap
  discriminants are `−156,−60,−156`. Ramsey trace makes every moving orbit a
  `C5` in the surviving `f=0,5` cases. For `f=0`, the required integral
  Seidel quotient has three first-row types and no completion after 56,755
  tested prefixes. For `f=5`, an eight-block incidence design plus a new
  triple-multiplicity bridge through `R(3,4)=R(4,3)=9` gives a solver-free
  `K5/I5` contradiction.
* **Order seven — support packing plus Rayleigh.** Cyclotomic parity leaves
  `f=3,17,31`. Handshake kills `f=3`; simultaneous original/complement support
  intersections have exact maximum family size six at `f=17`; and `f=31`
  yields a fixed-block test vector with quadratic value `−2790`, below the
  universal `−||v||²/4=−30/4` floor.
* **Order eleven and larger primes.** At `f=23`, a singleton-support class of
  at least eleven fixed vertices is independent. At `f=1`, quotient algebra
  is unique up to a swap; every one of the
  `10²·C(11,6)=46,200` cyclic two-orbit blocks has a `K4` or an `I5`
  (`44,000` primary `K4` witnesses, `2,200` fallback `I5` witnesses).
  Orders `13,17,19` fail fixed-graph handshake; `p≥23` cannot have the
  required even positive number of moving cycles. Cauchy's theorem converts
  the prime-order exclusions into the 2-group conclusion.
* **Independent proof certificate.** The producer is stdlib integer/bitset
  code. The checker imports no producer symbol: it reconstructs the
  fixed-point-free order-five matrix search, exhausts all 60 fixed-five block
  designs and their Ramsey bridges, derives the order-seven support/Rayleigh
  bounds from `(45,22,10,11)`, derives the order-eleven quotient, resweeps all
  46,200 cyclic blocks with a second graph builder and clique kernels, and
  enumerates every relevant odd prime through 43. The existing independent
  order-three checker is composed cryptographically.
* **Published-data corroboration.** Maksimović's 1,470,604-byte GAP/GRAPE
  payload (SHA-256
  `a53366d919f5d29876d07031a65c1bd722d05e88b59b39a04e9988c9162ebec0`)
  parses into 288 distinct labelled records. All 288 verify as
  `srg(45,22,10,11)`, admit the specified order-three action with nine fixed
  points and twelve 3-cycles, and contain both a `K5` and an `I5`. This is
  empirical corroboration only, not a catalog-completeness premise.
* **Verification and review.** The combined odd-prime, published-data,
  order-three, and Cayley suites passed **57/57** in **431.905 s**. Standalone
  order-three and odd-prime checkers and the live published-data replay all
  exited zero; new modules passed `py_compile`; all JSON artifacts/specs
  parsed. Independent current-snapshot code/certificate review returned
  **ACCEPTED — no P0/P1/P2 issue remains** after adding checker-only theorem
  derivations and correcting the exact order-seven support maximum from the
  loose bound eight to six.
* **Scope and next frontier.** The theorem is confined to Ramsey-good
  `srg(45,22,10,11)`. It leaves involutions and nontrivial 2-groups open, says
  nothing about non-strongly-regular Ramsey(5,5,45) graphs, and changes no
  Ramsey-number bound. The construction lane is now necessarily asymmetric
  with respect to every odd prime; surviving routes are the involution lane,
  VeriPB-certified non-local gluing, or unrestricted complementary SAT.

### KOBON (2026-08-23e) — HEREDITARY/SECTOR CUTS; FIRST STRETCHABILITY PROBE

* **New proved hereditary cut.** If \(W\) is any subset of the arrangement
  lines, every selected triangular face supported entirely in \(W\) remains a
  distinct triangular face after deleting the other lines. Hence its count is
  at most \(K(|W|)\); under exact selection of \(T\), the equivalent compact
  form requires at least \(T-K(|W|)\) selected faces to meet the deleted set.
  The \(n=12,T=39\) lane uses the non-circular inputs \(K(11)=32\) (Savchuk)
  and \(K(10)=25\) (the 2026-08-15 independently certified cube cover).
  `scratch/kobon/n12_gap_sector_deletion_t39.metadata.json` binds those
  sources explicitly; the \(K(10)\) cut did not depend on the then-pending
  face-monolith check.
* **New proved vertex-sector cut.** A fixed crossing pair supports at most four
  triangular faces; at a finite multipoint it supports at most two, and zero
  unless the pair is adjacent on one of the two arcs of the cyclic slope order.
  `engine.add_selected_face_sector_bounds` encodes the unconditional,
  multipoint-conditioned and nonadjacency forms. The direct-gap CLI now exposes
  `--sector-bounds` and repeatable `--sub-bound SIZE:UPPER`.

  aligned L20 target stratum, a three-character detector plus Weil's

### H10/Q (2026-08-23) — L25 SCALED SELF-COUPLINGS MOVE THE DYADIC WALL TO PARITY

* **Frontier result — PROVED local escape.** L24 had proved the two
  unscaled diagonal self-couplings uniformly empty on $\Phi$ over
  $\mathbb Q_2$.  L25 scales the tied norm witnesses by fixed rational
  numbers without adding a free variable.  With $u=\rho^2$, exact
  elimination gives two degree-two branch covers; replay verified the
  identities on **1,200** exact instances.
* **Complete parity split.** On $\Phi$, $(y,r)=(2X,\rho)$ is
  $2$-adically admissible exactly for even $s$, while
  $(y,r)=(2X,\rho/2)$ is admissible exactly for odd $s$; both statements
  are uniform in the declared scalar unit classes, in odd $b$, and in
  every $v_2(c)\ge4$.  The proof is a unit-root quartic/Hensel
  calculation: for odd $t$, $v_2(g'(t))=1$, and a root modulo $8$
  lifts; the two congruences are $g(1)\equiv2Abs^2$ and
  $g(1)\equiv2b(As^2-1)\pmod8$.  Their disjunction therefore covers all
  $s$ residues: L24's obstruction was an artifact of the unit scaling,
  not a universal coupled-family wall
  (`math/h10q/l25_scaled_coupling.py`;
  `math/h10q/data/l25_scaled_coupling.jsonl`).
* **Target place.** On the freely chosen unit-$b$ unramified reduction
  $c\equiv0\pmod w$, the residues
  $(s,A,X,\rho,b)\equiv(0,5,3,2,\kappa^{-2})$,
  $\kappa\in\{1,1/2\}$, satisfy both cover equations, and the Jacobian is
  the nonzero integer $-2400$, so Hensel lift works for every odd
  $w\ge7$.  Complete bounded scans show the streamlined proof does not
  lift at $w=3,5$; that small-prime analysis remains open, as does
  compatibility with preselected L20 target residues.
* **Real place.** The exact guarded bridge samples
  $(a,b,Z)=(1,3,-10)$ and $(3,4,-2/5)$ have eliminated quadratics with
  positive leading coefficient, negative linear coefficient, and
  nonnegative discriminant, hence positive real roots.  These are named
  parity-wise certificates; no all-parameter real theorem is claimed.
* **Strict scope.** No rational coupled point, no global member theorem,
  and no five-count improvement are claimed.  The bounded rational search
  found zero hits and is labelled EVIDENCE ONLY.  L24 remains true
  verbatim, and classical Schinzel H remains the sole conjectural input to
  the established conditional six-count.
* **Next frontier.** A five-shape route now needs both (i) control of
  the parity stratum of the free base parameter $s$ and (ii) a global
  rational-construction theorem for the two paired square conditions on
  the scaled square-branch cover.  Neither follows from the present
  Schinzel reduction.

### H10/Q (2026-08-22b) — L23/L24 SHARPEN THE UNCONDITIONAL FRONTIER; BOTH DIAGONALS DIE AT 2

* **Half-sieve theorem — PROVED, but not a member theorem.**  For the
  selected linear/octic sequence, the bad-root sieve has exact
  dimension $\kappa=1/2$ and Bombieri--Vinogradov level
  $D=X^{1/2}/(\log X)^B$.  The semilinear lower sieve at
  $z=X^{49/100}$ gives
  $\gg X/(\log X)^{3/2}$ prime members clean at every bad root prime
  below $z$.  Degree $8$ still permits $16$ large factors and
  reciprocity only forces an even bad count.  The first missing input is
  a parity-sensitive saving estimate for two large bad divisors
  $p_1p_2>D$, equivalently $R_{\rm bad}\le1$.  Even EH leaves eight;
  Kao gives only total $P_{12}$ after an AP adaptation
  (`math/h10q/l23_half_sieve.py`;
  `math/h10q/data/l23_half_sieve.jsonl`).
* **Capell/fibration frontier — PROVED at the stated scopes.**  On the
  canonical branch the tied quaternion is $(P(b),2b)$ and
  $2\theta\in K^2$ iff $P(u^2/2)$ is reducible; the automatic norm
  $N(2\theta)=256$ is not sufficient.  Odd $v_w(z)$ kills the square
  locus for every $a$, while the free
  $a\not\equiv1\pmod w$ refinement kills it on every selected cell.
  The resulting fixed-cell conic bundle has ten degenerate fibres in
  closed degrees $1+8+1$, non-split rank $10$, and
  $\operatorname{Br}(X)/\operatorname{Br}(\mathbb Q)=\mathbb Z/2$
  generated by $(A,b)$.  The checked low-rank fibration theorems do not
  apply.  Pre-existing even-$v_w(z)$, $w\mid s$ classes remain OPEN
  uniformly; recorded classes are settled only row by row
  (`math/h10q/l23_fibration.py`;
  `math/h10q/data/l23_fibration.jsonl`).
* **Norm/squareclass/multivariable walls — PROVED reductions and
  corrections.**  Exact matching and polynomial-in-$b$ norm sections
  miss $\Phi$; $P=\pm2b\,\square$ is dyadically empty;
  $P=A\,\mathrm{Norm}$ forces the wrong symbol; the fixed-$P$ square
  locus is genus $1$; and the first admissible factor ansatz

  branch systems are exact finite-flat degree-$8$ covers, so before

  diagonal five-count is FALSE and vacuous, not OPEN.**

### R55 (2026-08-22c) — ORDER-THREE AUTOMORPHISM LANE CLOSED IN THE RAMSEY CONFERENCE FAMILY

* **EXACT STRUCTURAL THEOREM:** a Ramsey-good `srg(45,22,10,11)` has no
  automorphism of order three. Spectral irreducibility leaves exactly the
  nonidentity fixed counts `3,9,15,21,27,33,39`; the preceding campaign
  excluded `3,9`, and the new moment argument excludes all five remaining
  counts. This changes no bound on `R(5,5)`.
* **The `f≥15` frontier collapses analytically:** with
  `c=(45−f)/3≤10`, spectral trace forces `c/2` triangle 3-orbits. For one
  triangle orbit, fixed support `s` and other-orbit quotient degrees `t_i`
  satisfy `Σt_i=20−s`, `ΣC(t_i,2)=9−s`, and `Σt_i²=38−3s`.
  Cauchy would require `(20−s)²≤(c−1)(38−3s)`, but at `c=10` the gap
  polynomial `s²−13s+58` has discriminant `−63`. The gap only grows as
  `c` decreases. This `f≥15` exclusion holds for every
  `srg(45,22,10,11)`, without the Ramsey hypothesis.
* **Full symmetry consequence:** `3∤|Aut(G)|` for a Ramsey-good candidate.
  Orbit-stabilizer and Cauchy's group theorem then exclude vertex-transitive
  Ramsey-good conference graphs on 45 vertices.
* **Evidence suite hardened:** `r55/data/order3_srg_frontier.json` is schema
  2 with disposition `ORDER3_AUTOMORPHISMS_EXCLUDED_FOR_RAMSEY_SRG`.
  The producer now gates the `f=9` degree-residue obstruction. The disjoint
  checker derives the adjacency polynomial, irreducibility discriminant,
  orbit-constant trace, triangle count, and both moment equations directly
  from `(45,22,10,11)`, rather than copying producer constants.
* **Review corrections:** the independent closure audits returned **SOUND**
  and observed that the new moment proof is Ramsey-free. A separate reviewer
  returned **THEOREM HOLDS** but found two P2 certificate weaknesses: a
  tautological degree-extreme helper/test and a checker that restated the
  trace/moment constants. Both were replaced by exact derived checks, with
  corruption regressions for the degree record and triangle trace; the
  focused re-review returned **ACCEPTED** with no remaining P0/P1/P2 finding.
* **Verification:** producer and disjoint checker both return the new terminal
  disposition with five large fixed counts and minimum integral Cauchy gap
  `16`; frontier suite **20/20 OK** in 5.880 s; combined Cayley+frontier suite
  **34/34 OK** in 245.706 s.
* **Source boundary:** Maksimović 2018 Table 4 has seven `Z3` orbit matrices
  and 288 `srg(45,22,10,11)` graphs from the sole viable `S3` distribution
  `(1,4,4,4)`, whose order-three element has `f=9`. No accessed source states
  the Ramsey-good order-three exclusion or the `f≥15` moment closure; this is
  not a novelty certification.

  `srg(45,22,10,11)` has no order-three automorphism with 0, 3, or 9 fixed

### R55 (2026-08-22) — ALL ORDER-45 CAYLEY CONSTRUCTIONS CLOSED EXACTLY

* **NEW EXACT CLASS EXCLUSION:** no undirected Cayley graph on 45 vertices is
  Ramsey(5,5). This is deliberately not an upper bound on `R(5,5)`.

  Every class has an explicit monochromatic K5 witness.

### KOBON (2026-08-22d) — DIRECT-GAP ENCODING + 2005 PRIOR-ART CORRECTION

* **Priority correction:** Rasukaru's Japanese web essays (22 Sep--8 Oct
  2005; primary Wayback pages read directly, Honma attribution verified)
  already give geometric arguments for K(6)=7, K(8)=15, K(10)=25 and
  K(11)=32, and state an even-n bound giving 54 at n=14. Therefore our ladder
  claims **first machine-checkable all-degeneracy certificates known to us**,
  not first mathematical determinations. The part-3 generalisation itself
  labels two perturbation reductions “probably” harmless; it is informal,
  non-refereed, and has no proof certificate.
* **The missed global mechanism:** Rasukaru's sequential/per-line charge is
  not Clément--Bader's false “at most two shared pairs per multipoint” claim.
  It motivates the face-specific square penalty
  `3F <= n(n-2)-2Q-sum_p(k_p-2)^2`. The saturated flower does not refute it:
  closing the outer triangle forces compensating multipoints at its vertices.
  No published repair was found. An exact 4,000-arrangement / 109,104-triple
  audit across five degeneracy modes has zero direct-gap mismatches and zero
  square-penalty violations; minimum slack 0. Evidence only, not theorem.
* **Universal direct-gap criterion proved and audited:** a nondegenerate
  triple is a triangular arrangement face iff no outside-line intersection is
  strictly between its two vertices on any side. Ties at vertices are allowed.
  Direct X-order clauses remove SA/SB, B, IN, TC and R1--R4. Independent exact
  sign-straddle comparison: 109,104 triples, zero mismatches. A `(7,12)`
  direct-gap DRAT is `s VERIFIED` (4,412 formula clauses / 14,545 lemmas in
  core).
* **Scale reduction:** universal `(12,39)` = 288,179 vars / 653,828 clauses
  with per-line counters, or 45,014 / 164,537 global-only; `(14,55)` =
  858,782 / 1,891,433 or 128,612 / 424,240. All four fronts are live.
* **Certified n=12 no-concurrency theorem:** capacity reduces a 39-face
  no-multipoint arrangement to Q=0 or Q=1. Q=0 is simple and Blanc caps it at
  37. The canonical Q=1 branch has **two independently DRAT-verified
  certificates**. Primary, no extra symmetry: 20,895 vars / 111,753 clauses;
  2,437,053,136-byte proof; 19,802 formula clauses + 6,539,084 lemmas in
  core; 412,704,063 resolution steps. Compact cross-check with valid WLOG
  `X(0,2,3)`: 111,754 clauses; 759,328,450-byte proof; 126,043,830 steps.
  Hash-bound dual manifest:
  `scratch/kobon/n12/n12_q1_gap_certificate.json`. Therefore every
  hypothetical 12-line/39-face arrangement has a finite multipoint. This does
  not yet prove K(12)=38. The analogous n=14 Q1 cube (44,399 / 245,822)
  remains live.
* **Controls:** exact n=10 Q1 certificate is pair `{0,1}`, 25 faces, zero
  crossings; gap SAT at 25 (5.7s), UNSAT at 26 (1.1s). Direct-gap SAT/UNSAT
  controls pass at K(n)/K(n)+1 for every n=5..9. Paper corrected and compiles
  to 14 pages after the Rasukaru attribution.


### KOBON (2026-08-22c) — SATURATED FLOWERS + STRONG FACE FRONTIERS

* **Clément--Bader Lemma 1 proof step falsified in its own face convention.**
  For every k>=3, k concurrent cevians through an outer triangle partition it
  into 2k triangular arrangement faces; all 2k radial elementary segments are
  shared by the adjacent pair of faces. The draft says one multipoint belongs
  to at most two shared-side pairs. This invalidates the proof step, not the
  numerical bound itself.
* **Exact artifacts:** `scratch/kobon/flower_counterexamples.py` and persisted
  JSON verify a triple flower with 6 face triangles / 6 shared sides and a
  quadruple flower with 8 / 8, using Fraction arithmetic,
  `verify_selection`, an independent side-use census, and zero
  line/triangle-interior incidences. This proves the capacity coefficient
  k(k-4) is locally sharp: a k-fold merge loses k(k-2) gaps and all 2k
  double-use credits can occur simultaneously. The missing mod-6 unit must be
  a **global interaction/parity obstruction**, not a pointwise charge.

  rules and removes all TC variables. The added side-order condition is
  redundant on realizable triangular faces but strengthens the relaxation.
  SAT controls pass at every known optimum n=5..9; next-value controls are
  UNSAT. Runtime: (8,16) 31.6s -> 16.7s, (9,22) 388s -> 155s.

  one vertex of t" (two would force r to be a side line, excluded by r not in

  excluded.

### KOBON (2026-08-22) - THE CONVENTIONS COLLAPSE: crossings never help, so K = K_gen

* **THEOREM (collapse), proved and machine-checked**: for every arrangement A,
  `face(A) = broad(A)`; hence **`K_gen(n) = K(n)` for all n**. Proof is two
  steps:
  * *Corner-descent lemma*: every triangle bounded by three lines of an
    arrangement contains a triangular FACE. Induct on the number of lines
    meeting the interior; a chord either cuts off a corner (leaving a triangle
    on two old lines + the chord) or runs from a vertex to the opposite side
    (splitting into two such triangles); the piece loses that chord, so the
    parameter strictly drops.
  * *Exchange*: replace a crossed member of an interior-disjoint family by a
    face inside it; size preserved, crossed count drops. Iterate.
* **Machine check** (`scratch/kobon/collapse_verify.py`): 872 exact-rational
  arrangements at n<=7 over six degeneracy modes (generic, parallel pair,
  parallel triple, triple point, quadruple point, two triple points);
  **13,017 triangles all descend to a face** (depths 0-4); **zero**
  arrangements with face < broad. Independent sweep
  (`convention_sweep.py`): 1,120 arrangements at n=5,6, zero gaps.
* **HONEST DOWNGRADE**: this retires the convention hierarchy that the prior
  session's paper was built on. Consequences:
  * The ceiling `K_gen(n) <= floor((n-2)(5n-3)/12)` is **superseded** -
    weaker than `floor(n(n-2)/3)` at every n>=4, since 5n-3 > 4n. Kept only
    as a capacity-machinery corollary.
  * `K_gen(8)=15`, `K_gen(9)=21` are **not new values** - they equal the
    classical K(8), K(9). The DRAT ladder is an independent all-degeneracy
    *verification* of the classical Kobon numbers, not new mathematics.
  * The n=13 T=48 "separation probe" and the n=14 T=56 monolith are
    **provably redundant** (48 > floor(13*11/3) = 47; T=56 is implied by
    T=55). Both frozen, state preserved.
* **POINTWISE FALSE**: the collapse equates maxima, not optima. Crossed
  optima genuinely exist - at n=4, 296 of 300 random exact-rational
  arrangements have an optimal 2-family using a crossed triangle; at n=6 the
  exact arrangement in `scratch/kobon/discoveries/n6_convention_separation.json`
  attains the record 7 with `012` crossed by line 4, and descent recovers the
  face optimum by swapping `012 -> 014`. SAT-certified companion: forcing a
  crossing into an optimal family is SAT at n=4,6 and UNSAT at n=5,7,8.
* **SOUND SEARCH WLOG** (`scratch/kobon/faces_only.py`): crossings may be
  forbidden outright. Validated against known values - UNSAT at (7,12),
  (8,16), (9,22); ~2x faster than the broad encoding (n=8: 75s -> 31.6s;
  n=9: 710s -> 388s).
* **RETARGETED FRONTIER** (the rigorous K-windows, with OEIS's simple-only
  upper bounds discarded): n=11 closed by Savchuk; n=13 closed (FK bound =
  construction 47); **n=12 open at [38,39]** - the smallest genuinely open
  case, decided by the single instance (12,39), now running; n=14 open at
  [54,55], decided by (14,55) - `n14_faces_t55.cnf` (1,349,454 vars /
  7,829,001 clauses) launched; n=18 [93,95]; n=20 [116,119].
* **DISK**: guard fired exactly as designed -
  `GUARD-STOP pid=88776 rc=0 state=T free_kib=15678924`. With approval,
  255 GB of partial DRAT proofs for now-redundant runs were removed
  (n11_monolith_t33.drat 91 GB, n14c-q0tp1 75 GB, n14c-q1tp1d 82 GB); the
  blocks are currently pinned by Time Machine local snapshots, which macOS

### R55 MIXED (2026-08-21) — level-2 pair lift executed and CLOSED; a retraction

* **THE PAIR LIFT IS CLOSED.** The falsification criterion recorded at the end
  of the mixed campaign (note §6.5) was run and fired. Exact optima of the
  level-2 pair-coupled programme, maximised over every feasible integer edge
  count (exactly `e in [454,536]`): `total_deficiency` 360 (frozen 360),
  `degree20_count` **41** (frozen 14310/349, i.e. improved by exactly 1/349),
  `deficiency_ge8_count` 45 (frozen 45). No route reaches its edge
  (315, 1, 1). Disposition `MIXED_PAIR_LIFT_CLOSED`. **No R(5,5) bound
  claimed.** Levels 1 and 2 of the first exact basis at n=45 are now both
  closed; the recorded residual tiers (VeriPB gluing, then srg(45,22,10,11)
  complementary SAT) are the only remaining routes.
* **WHY — `excess_balance` IS the degree-squared identity.** Pure double
  counting gives `sum_{u~v} deg(u) = e + e(G[N(v)]) - e(G[D(v)])` per vertex and
  `sum_v deg(v)^2 = 45e + sum_v e(G[N(v)]) - sum_v e(G[D(v)])` in aggregate
  (verified on all 2,131,018 labelled graphs to n=7 plus randoms at n=12,20,45;
  independently re-verified by a second agent over all graphs on n=4,5,6,
  209,184 per-vertex checks, 0 violations). Adjoining it gains nothing: the repo's
  z stratum is the *complement* of the anti-neighbourhood, so the true interior
  count is `e_y = C(m,2) - e_z`, and with that substitution
  `2d^2 - 45d - 2e_x + 2e_y == excess_balance(s)` exactly on all 3215 states.
  Exact rank stays 8 before and after. This retires every handshake-,
  assortativity- and degree-correlation repair at the aggregate level — including
  the one the campaign note itself proposed. Only the per-degree-class form is
  finer, and it is worth 1/349.
* **RETRACTION (mine, mid-session).** Substituting the recorded `e_z` for
  `e(G[D(v)])` gives a row that IS independent of the frozen basis (rank 8->9)
  and appears to cut hard: 360->4905/14, 14310/349->225/11, 45->4905/112, and
  under integrality of the degree distribution 349/20/43 — each with matching
  exact dual certificates AND exact primal witnesses. All retracted: the row
  misreads the stratum convention. Exact certificates prove optimality *for the
  programme written*, not soundness of the programme. A second trap was hit and
  caught the same way: reading the non-adjacent codegree bound
  `lam <= d+d'-30` as a lower bound makes every e infeasible and so manufactures
  a false refutation of R(5,5)=45. Both are now pinned by regression tests
  (`test_invalid_ez_row_is_the_trap`, `test_nonadjacent_bound_is_an_upper_bound`).
* **TASK 4 LANDED** (the plan's last open item, delegated): disjoint checker
  `r55/src/check_mixed_certificate.py` + 17 tests. Imports only stdlib plus
  `check_ramsey.parse_graph6_line`/`popcount`; second independently written motif
  kernel cross-validated by a combinations-only reference on all 33,867 labelled
  graphs of orders 1..6; `--sweep` re-swept 8,500,211 graphs across 53 classes in
  333 s with every per-class window agreeing; independent histogram-DP
  reconstruction of q and the g endpoints (no frozen-m4 state comparison);
  all 3215 F intervals re-derived; least-alpha canonicality enforced. Scoped gap
  recorded verbatim in its docstring: outer windows for catalog-missing classes
  are validated for schema, combinatorial caps and catalog containment but the
  R1-R5 envelope LP is not re-run, so h and F endpoints in missing strata remain
  conditional on those hash-pinned windows.
* New: `r55/src/pair_lift_mixed.py`, `r55/tests/test_pair_lift_mixed.py` (18
  tests). Verified: pair-lift suite 18/18 OK (131 s); checker suite 17/17 OK
  (13 s); `pair_lift_mixed.py` CLI rc 0 printing `MIXED_PAIR_LIFT_CLOSED`;
  `check_mixed_certificate.py` CLI rc 0 printing
  `MIXED EVIDENCE VERIFIED: MIXED_NO_CUT_IN_FROZEN_BASIS`. Paper rebuilt to 8

  = Clement-Bader's actual object: their Kobon triangle is "realized by 3

  exact. Audit: it pairs a NON-simple construction (two shared-line triple

* **NEW BOUND covering `K_gen`**: capacity + audited face budget give
  `6T + 4C + 5Q + sum_k N_k (k(k-4) + 3*C(k-1,2)) <= n(n-2) + 3*C(n-1,2)`,

code except the collar floor, identical splitter, same machine), so it measures
what every earlier plan could only guess. Margin
$1.6298\,(c^*-t)$ fell by $1.55703\times$; committed trees grew by:

|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | 21,135,611 | 21,891,091 | 1.0357 | 0.079 |

| 2 | 23,486,265 | 24,563,611 | 1.0459 | 0.101 |
